						<svg class="size-10 text-green-600" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><desc>Check circle alternate</desc><path d="M6 13.223 8.45 16.7a1.049 1.049 0 0 0 1.707 0.051L18 6.828" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M0.75 11.999a11.25 11.25 0 1 0 22.5 0 11.25 11.25 0 1 0 -22.5 0Z" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path></svg>					</div>

					<!-- Reward value display -->
					<div>
						<p class="text-base text-gray-600 mt-2 mb-0.5">
							You have received						</p>
						<div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
							<div class="size-8 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-gray-700">
								<svg class="size-8" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M21 11.25v8.25a1.5 1.5 0 01-1.5 1.5H4.5a1.5 1.5 0 01-1.5-1.5v-8.25M12 4.875A2.625 2.625 0 109.375 7.5H12m0-2.625V7.5m0-2.625A2.625 2.625 0 1114.625 7.5H12m0 0V21m-8.625-9.75h18c.621 0 1.125-.504 1.125-1.125v-1.5c0-.621-.504-1.125-1.125-1.125h-18c-.621 0-1.125.504-1.125 1.125v1.5c0 .621.504 1.125 1.125 1.125z"></path></svg>							</div>
							<div class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900" x-text="rewardTitle">
															</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>

				<!-- Usage Instructions -->
				<div x-show="couponCode" class="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center mb-2 px-2">
					<p>Use at checkout on your first order</p>
				</div>

				<!-- Coupon Code Display (if coupon code available) -->
				<div x-show="couponCode" class="mb-4">
					<div x-data="{ isCheckmark: false }" class="flex rounded-lg bg-white outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200">
						<input x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, null, false)" type="text" readonly class="block min-w-0 grow px-5 py-3 text-sm text-gray-900 focus:outline-none sm:text-sm/6 text-ellipsis cursor-pointer font-mono" x-ref="couponCode" x-bind:value="couponCode">

						<div class="flex">
								<button x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data)" class="py-3 px-3 relative transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer" x-bind:class="{ 'hover:text-gray-900 text-gray-500': !isCheckmark}" x-ref="copyButton">
									<div class="relative size-6">
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out" x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-0 scale-95' : 'opacity-100 scale-100'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="-1 -1 24 24"><desc>Copy to clipboard icon</desc><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M17.875 6.645833333333333h2.520833333333333c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666V17.875" stroke-width="2"></path><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M0.6875 1.6041666666666665c0 -0.5062658333333333 0.41041 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666h12.833333333333332c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666v-12.833333333333332Z" stroke-width="2"></path></svg>										</div>
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out" x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-100 scale-100' : 'opacity-0 scale-95'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>										</div>
									</div>
								</button>
							</div>
					</div>
				</div>



				<!-- Apply Coupon Button - Only show when NOT applied and NOT failed due to minimum spend -->
				<div x-show="couponCode &amp;&amp; !couponApplied &amp;&amp; !productAddedButCouponFailed" x-cloak class="mb-4">
					<button x-on:click="applyCouponToCart()" x-bind:disabled="applyingCoupon" x-bind:class="{
							'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed': applyingCoupon,
							'hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover': !applyingCoupon
						}" class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center">
						<span class="[grid-area:stack] flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity duration-200" x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': applyingCoupon, 'visible': !applyingCoupon }">
							<!-- Dynamic text based on reward type, cart state, and minimum spend -->
							<template x-if="rewardType === 'free_product'">
								<span x-text="freeProductButtonText"></span>
							</template>
							<template x-if="rewardType !== 'free_product'">
								<span>
							<span x-show="isWooCommerce">Apply to cart</span>
							<span x-show="!isWooCommerce">Apply coupon</span>
								</span>
							</template>
						</span>
						<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: applyingCoupon !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: applyingCoupon === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>					</button>
					<div x-show="couponError &amp;&amp; !productAddedButCouponFailed" class="text-sm mt-2 text-red-500" x-text="couponErrorMessage"></div>
				</div>

				<!-- Success Message - Show when coupon applied -->
				<div x-show="couponApplied" x-cloak class="mb-1 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-emerald-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
					<span> applied</span>
				</div>

								<div x-show="productAddedButCouponFailed" x-cloak class="mt-3 mb-3 text-sm w-full space-y-2">
					<div x-show="productWasNewlyAdded" class="text-center text-emerald-600 font-medium">
						Product added to cart					</div>
										<template> 0"&gt;
						<div class="text-center text-gray-600 px-2" x-text="'Almost there — add $' + remainingToUnlock + ' more to unlock'"></div>
					</template>
				</div>

				<!-- Buttons when coupon failed but product added -->
				<template x-if="productAddedButCouponFailed">
					<div>
						<!-- Primary CTA - Continue Shopping -->
						<a x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl" x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }" class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block">
							Continue shopping						</a>

						<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
						<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
							<a x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : 'https://myaminolab.com/cart/'" x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }" class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block">
								View cart							</a>
						</template>
					</div>
				</template>

				<!-- Buttons when coupon is applied successfully -->
				<template x-if="couponApplied &amp;&amp; !isCartEmpty">
					<div>
						<!-- Primary CTA - Go to Checkout -->
						<a x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : 'https://myaminolab.com/checkout/'" class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block" x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }">
							Go to checkout						</a>

						<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
						<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
							<a x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : 'https://myaminolab.com/cart/'" x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }" class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block">
								View cart							</a>
						</template>
					</div>
				</template>

				<!-- Button when coupon is applied but cart is empty -->
				<template x-if="couponApplied &amp;&amp; isCartEmpty">
					<div>
						<!-- Primary CTA - Shop Now -->
						<a x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl" x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }" class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block">
							Shop now						</a>
						<!-- No secondary button when cart is empty -->
					</div>
				</template>
			</div>

			<!-- Form State -->
			<div x-show="!showSuccess">
				<!-- Claim instruction -->
				<p class="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center mb-2 px-2">
					Enter your email to claim it now.				</p>

				<!-- Email Form -->
				<form x-on:submit.prevent="if (!isAdminPreview &amp;&amp; !isDemo) { claimReward() }" class="mb-3">
							<div style="position:absolute;left:-9999px;top:-9999px;opacity:0">
			<label class="sr-only">
				Leave this field empty			</label>
			<input type="text" name="rwp_website" x-model="formData.rwp_website" value="">
		</div>
							<div class="mb-4">

						<label for="claim-email" class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5 text-left sr-only">Email</label>
						<input type="email" id="claim-email" x-model="formData.email" class="bg-white text-base w-full px-3 py-3 rounded-lg outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200 placeholder:text-gray-400 focus:outline-2 focus:-outline-offset-2 focus:outline-blue-500" placeholder="you@example.com" required>

					</div>

					<!-- Error State -->
					<div x-show="showError" x-transition:enter="transition-opacity duration-300" x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0" x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100" class="mb-4">
						<div class="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
							<div class="flex items-center">
								<svg class="size-5 text-red-400 mr-3 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
									<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path>
								</svg>
								<span class="text-sm text-red-700 text-left" x-html="errorMessage"></span>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>

					<button type="submit" x-bind:disabled="isSubmitting" x-bind:class="isSubmitting ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : (isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? '!cursor-not-allowed' : 'hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover'" class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center">
						<span class="[grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': isSubmitting, 'visible': !isSubmitting }">Claim your gift</span>
						<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: isSubmitting !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: isSubmitting === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>					</button>

				</form>
			</div>

		</div>








				</div>


			<div
		x-show="currentPanel === 'ways-to-earn'"
		x-bind:class="'relative h-full flex flex-col overflow-hidden ' + (currentPanel === 'ways-to-earn' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
		x-data="{ panelAtTop: true, panelAtBottom: false }"
		x-effect="if (currentPanel === 'ways-to-earn') { const el = $el.querySelector('.overflow-y-auto'); if (el && el.scrollHeight > 0) { panelAtBottom = el.scrollTop + el.clientHeight >= el.scrollHeight - 5; } }"
	>
		<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 py-6 pb-32 text-rwp-widget-text"
			@scroll="panelAtTop = $el.scrollTop < 5; panelAtBottom = $el.scrollTop + $el.clientHeight >= $el.scrollHeight - 5"
		>

			<h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-3">Ways to earn</h2>

								<div
					x-show="showWelcomeBanner"
					role="status"
					class="mt-3 mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-emerald-800"
					style="display: none;"
				>
					<svg class="size-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>					<div class="flex-1">
						<span x-show="welcomeBannerData.signupPoints > 0">
							Welcome, 							<span x-text="welcomeBannerData.memberName || ''"></span>!
						</span>
						<span x-show="welcomeBannerData.signupPoints === 0 || !welcomeBannerData.signupPoints">
							Welcome, 							<span x-text="welcomeBannerData.memberName || ''"></span>—you&#039;re in.						</span>
					</div>
					<div class="ml-auto" x-show="welcomeBannerData.signupPoints > 0 || false">
						+<span x-text="(welcomeBannerData.signupPoints || 0).toLocaleString()"></span>
						<span x-text="(welcomeBannerData.signupPoints || 0) == 1 ? 'Point' : 'Points'"></span>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- Error Notification for Guest Upgrade -->
		<div x-show="errorMessage" class="mt-3 mb-2 rounded-lg bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-800" role="alert" style="display: none;">
						<span x-text="errorMessage"></span>
		</div>

		<div class="divide-y divide-gray-50">
																<div
							class="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto] items-center gap-4 py-4"
													>
							<div class="size-8 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-rwp-widget-icon-text">
								<svg class="size-8" viewBox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
			<desc>
				Place an order icon
			</desc>
			<path d="M19.049 6.75H4.936A1.4 1.4 0 0 0 3.5 7.907l-2 13.915a1.357 1.357 0 0 0 1.437 1.428h18.111a1.356 1.356 0 0 0 1.436 -1.428l-2 -13.915a1.4 1.4 0 0 0 -1.435 -1.157Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="M15.662 3.807A3.765 3.765 0 0 0 11.976 0.75h0A3.763 3.763 0 0 0 8.3 3.788" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="M15.923 12.845a1.9 1.9 0 0 0 -2.752 0l-1.179 1.219 -1.179 -1.219a1.9 1.9 0 0 0 -2.751 0 2.06 2.06 0 0 0 0 2.845l3.512 3.63a0.578 0.578 0 0 0 0.837 0l3.512 -3.63a2.062 2.062 0 0 0 0 -2.845Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			</svg>							</div>
							<div class="min-w-0">
																	<div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Make a purchase</div>
																								<div class="text-xs text-gray-500">5 Points for every $1 spent</div>
													</div>

						<!-- CTA Button Section -->
													<!-- Non-signup actions with CTA -->
							<div class="justify-self-end" x-show="!!memberId" x-cloak>
																	<!-- Regular CTA with URL - visible only to members -->
									<a
										x-show="!!memberId"
										x-cloak
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : 'https://myaminolab.com/shop/'"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
										class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 py-2 px-4 grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center min-h-[36px] min-w-[5rem]"
									>
										Shop									</a>
															</div>
											</div>
															<div
							class="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto] items-center gap-4 py-4"
														x-data="{
								isCompleted: false,
								signupPoints: 50,
								completedDate: ''
							}"
							x-init="
								if (!isCompleted && $root.welcomeBannerData && !!$root.welcomeBannerData.signupPoints) {
									isCompleted = true;
									signupPoints = $root.welcomeBannerData.signupPoints;
									completedDate = 'today';
								}
							"
							x-on:member-joined.window="
								if ($event.detail && !!$event.detail.signupPoints) {
									isCompleted = true;
									signupPoints = $event.detail.signupPoints;
									completedDate = 'today';
								}
							"
													>
							<div class="size-8 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-rwp-widget-icon-text">
								<svg class="size-8" viewBox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
			<desc>
				Create account icon
			</desc>
			<path d="m15.75 9.749 -3.981 5.308a0.75 0.75 0 0 1 -1.13 0.08L8.25 12.749" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="M10.73 1.357a1.63 1.63 0 0 1 2.54 0l1.512 1.881a1.629 1.629 0 0 0 1.446 0.6l2.4 -0.261a1.63 1.63 0 0 1 1.8 1.8l-0.261 2.4a1.629 1.629 0 0 0 0.6 1.446l1.881 1.512a1.63 1.63 0 0 1 0 2.54l-1.887 1.505a1.632 1.632 0 0 0 -0.6 1.447l0.261 2.4a1.629 1.629 0 0 1 -1.8 1.8l-2.4 -0.261a1.628 1.628 0 0 0 -1.446 0.6L13.27 22.64a1.629 1.629 0 0 1 -2.54 0l-1.511 -1.88a1.631 1.631 0 0 0 -1.447 -0.6l-2.4 0.261a1.628 1.628 0 0 1 -1.8 -1.8l0.261 -2.4a1.631 1.631 0 0 0 -0.6 -1.447l-1.88 -1.511a1.629 1.629 0 0 1 0 -2.54l1.88 -1.512a1.628 1.628 0 0 0 0.6 -1.446l-0.261 -2.4a1.629 1.629 0 0 1 1.8 -1.8l2.4 0.261a1.632 1.632 0 0 0 1.447 -0.6Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			</svg>							</div>
							<div class="min-w-0">
																	<div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Create an account</div>
																									<!-- Show points when not completed, earned date when completed -->
									<div class="text-xs text-gray-500">
										<span x-show="!isCompleted" x-text="signupPoints.toLocaleString() + ' ' + (signupPoints == 1 ? 'Point' : 'Points')"></span>
										<span x-show="isCompleted" x-cloak>Earned <span x-text="completedDate"></span></span>
									</div>
														</div>

						<!-- CTA Button Section -->
																				<!-- Signup action: Always render completion indicator in DOM for dynamic updates -->
							<div class="justify-self-end" x-show="isCompleted" x-cloak>
																<!-- Completion indicator - always in DOM, controlled by Alpine -->
								<div
									x-show="isCompleted"
									x-cloak
									class="text-emerald-600 font-semibold text-sm"
									x-bind:aria-label="'You earned ' + signupPoints.toLocaleString() + ' ' + (signupPoints == 1 ? 'Point' : 'Points') + ' for creating your account'"
								>
									+<span x-text="signupPoints.toLocaleString()"></span>
								</div>
							</div>
											</div>
						</div>

		</div>

		<!-- Top scroll fade -->
		<div x-show="!panelAtTop"
			class="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 h-8 z-10"
			style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>
		<!-- Bottom scroll fade — hidden when guest join section is visible -->
		<div x-show="!panelAtBottom && memberId !== 0"
			class="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-12 z-10"
			style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>

				<div x-show="memberId === 0">
		<!-- Fade Overlay -->
		<div
			class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-24 pointer-events-none"
			style="background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), var(--color-rwp-widget-bg) 20%, transparent);"
			x-data="{ hideJoinSection: false }"
			x-on:member-joined.window="hideJoinSection = true"
			x-show="!hideJoinSection"
		></div>

		<!-- Sticky Join Section -->
		<div
			class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-white px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-100"
			x-data="{ hideJoinSection: false, isLoggedIn: false }"
			x-on:member-joined.window="hideJoinSection = true"
			x-show="!hideJoinSection"
		>
	<div>
	<button
		type="button"
		x-on:click="window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-register-form'))"
		class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center"
		style="--tw-ring-color: var(--rwp-accent);"
	>
			Join now	</button>
		</div>
				<div x-show="!isLoggedIn" x-cloak class="mt-3 text-center text-rwp-widget-text">
					<p class="text-sm">
						Already have an account?						<button
							x-on:click="navigateToPanel('main', { resetScroll: true }); $dispatch('show-login-form');"
							class="font-medium underline text-rwp-accent cursor-pointer"
						>Sign in</button>
					</p>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
					</div>

			<div
		x-show="currentPanel === 'birthday-picker'"
		x-bind:class="'relative h-full flex flex-col ' + (currentPanel === 'birthday-picker' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
	>
		<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 py-6 text-rwp-widget-text">

			<!-- Success State -->
			<div x-show="birthdaySuccess" x-transition class="text-center">
				<div class="flex justify-center mb-3">
					<svg class="size-10 text-green-600" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><desc>Check circle alternate</desc><path d="M6 13.223 8.45 16.7a1.049 1.049 0 0 0 1.707 0.051L18 6.828" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M0.75 11.999a11.25 11.25 0 1 0 22.5 0 11.25 11.25 0 1 0 -22.5 0Z" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path></svg>				</div>
				<h3 class="text-lg font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-1">Birthday set!</h3>
				<template x-if="birthdayRewardDate">
					<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-6">You&#039;ll earn <span x-text="birthdayPoints + ' ' + currencyPlural"></span> on <span x-text="birthdayRewardDate"></span></p>
				</template>
				<template x-if="!birthdayRewardDate">
					<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-6">You&#039;ll earn bonus points on your special day!</p>
				</template>
				<button
					type="button"
					x-on:click="resetBirthdayPicker(); navigateToPanel('ways-to-earn')"
					class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center"
				>
					Keep earning				</button>
			</div>

			<!-- Form State -->
			<div x-show="!birthdaySuccess">
				<div class="text-center mb-4">
					<div class="flex justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
						<svg class="size-10 text-rwp-widget-icon-text" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
			<desc>Birthday cake icon</desc>
			<path d="M3.08108 11.7027H20.9189C20.9189 11.7027 22.7027 11.7027 22.7027 13.4865V21.2162C22.7027 21.2162 22.7027 23 20.9189 23H3.08108C3.08108 23 1.2973 23 1.2973 21.2162V13.4865C1.2973 13.4865 1.2973 11.7027 3.08108 11.7027Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
			<path d="M12 9.02703V11.7027" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
			<path d="M13.7838 3.67568C13.7838 4.14876 13.5959 4.60248 13.2613 4.937C12.9268 5.27153 12.4731 5.45946 12 5.45946C11.5269 5.45946 11.0732 5.27153 10.7387 4.937C10.4041 4.60248 10.2162 4.14876 10.2162 3.67568C10.2162 2.68865 12 1 12 1C12 1 13.7838 2.68865 13.7838 3.67568Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
			<path d="M19.1351 9.02703V11.7027" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
			<path d="M20.9189 3.67568C20.9189 4.14876 20.731 4.60248 20.3965 4.937C20.0619 5.27153 19.6082 5.45946 19.1351 5.45946C18.662 5.45946 18.2083 5.27153 17.8738 4.937C17.5393 4.60248 17.3514 4.14876 17.3514 3.67568C17.3514 2.68865 19.1351 1 19.1351 1C19.1351 1 20.9189 2.68865 20.9189 3.67568Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
			<path d="M4.86486 9.02703V11.7027" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
			<path d="M6.64865 3.67568C6.64865 4.14876 6.46071 4.60248 6.12619 4.937C5.79167 5.27153 5.33795 5.45946 4.86486 5.45946C4.39178 5.45946 3.93806 5.27153 3.60354 4.937C3.26901 4.60248 3.08108 4.14876 3.08108 3.67568C3.08108 2.68865 4.86486 1 4.86486 1C4.86486 1 6.64865 2.68865 6.64865 3.67568Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
			<path d="M1.2973 17.0184C1.57132 17.4845 1.96234 17.8709 2.43166 18.1394C2.90097 18.4079 3.43228 18.5492 3.97297 18.5492C4.51367 18.5492 5.04498 18.4079 5.51429 18.1394C5.9836 17.8709 6.37463 17.4845 6.64865 17.0184C6.92267 17.4845 7.3137 17.8709 7.78301 18.1394C8.25232 18.4079 8.78363 18.5492 9.32432 18.5492C9.86502 18.5492 10.3963 18.4079 10.8656 18.1394C11.335 17.8709 11.726 17.4845 12 17.0184C12.274 17.4845 12.665 17.8709 13.1344 18.1394C13.6037 18.4079 14.135 18.5492 14.6757 18.5492C15.2164 18.5492 15.7477 18.4079 16.217 18.1394C16.6863 17.8709 17.0773 17.4845 17.3514 17.0184C17.6254 17.4845 18.0164 17.8709 18.4857 18.1394C18.955 18.4079 19.4863 18.5492 20.027 18.5492C20.5677 18.5492 21.099 18.4079 21.5683 18.1394C22.0377 17.8709 22.4287 17.4845 22.7027 17.0184" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
			</svg>					</div>
					<h3 class="text-lg font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-3 text-center" x-text="birthdayIsEditing ? 'Update your birthday' : 'When is your birthday?'"></h3>
					<p class="text-sm text-gray-500">
						<template x-if="birthdayRewardPreview">
							<span>You&#039;ll earn <span x-text="birthdayPoints + ' ' + currencyPlural"></span> on <span x-text="birthdayRewardPreview.rewardDate"></span></span>
						</template>
						<template x-if="!birthdayRewardPreview">
							<span>You&#039;ll earn <span x-text="birthdayPoints + ' ' + currencyPlural"></span> on your special day!</span>
						</template>
					</p>
				</div>

								<form x-on:submit.prevent="submitBirthday()">
					<div class="grid gap-3 mb-4 grid-cols-[1fr_6rem]">
												<div class="grid grid-cols-1">
							<label for="rwp-birthday-month" class="sr-only">Month</label>
							<select
								id="rwp-birthday-month"
								x-model="birthdayMonth"
								x-on:change="if (!birthdayDay) { setTimeout(() => $refs.birthdayDaySelect?.focus(), 300) }"
								class="col-start-1 row-start-1 w-full appearance-none bg-white text-base px-3 py-3 pr-8 rounded-lg outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200 focus:outline-2 focus:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
								required
							>
								<option value="">Month</option>
								<option value="1">January</option>
								<option value="2">February</option>
								<option value="3">March</option>
								<option value="4">April</option>
								<option value="5">May</option>
								<option value="6">June</option>
								<option value="7">July</option>
								<option value="8">August</option>
								<option value="9">September</option>
								<option value="10">October</option>
								<option value="11">November</option>
								<option value="12">December</option>
							</select>
							<svg class="pointer-events-none col-start-1 row-start-1 mr-3 size-4 self-center justify-self-end text-gray-500" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4.22 6.22a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0L8 8.94l2.72-2.72a.75.75 0 1 1 1.06 1.06l-3.25 3.25a.75.75 0 0 1-1.06 0L4.22 7.28a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.06Z" clip-rule="evenodd" /></svg>						</div>
												<div class="grid grid-cols-1">
							<label for="rwp-birthday-day" class="sr-only">Day</label>
							<select
								id="rwp-birthday-day"
								x-model="birthdayDay"
								x-ref="birthdayDaySelect"
								class="col-start-1 row-start-1 w-full appearance-none bg-white text-base px-3 py-3 pr-8 rounded-lg outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200 focus:outline-2 focus:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
								required
							>
								<option value="">Day</option>
								<template x-for="d in birthdayDaysInMonth" :key="d">
									<option :value="d" :selected="String(d) === birthdayDay" x-text="d"></option>
								</template>
							</select>
							<svg class="pointer-events-none col-start-1 row-start-1 mr-3 size-4 self-center justify-self-end text-gray-500" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4.22 6.22a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0L8 8.94l2.72-2.72a.75.75 0 1 1 1.06 1.06l-3.25 3.25a.75.75 0 0 1-1.06 0L4.22 7.28a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.06Z" clip-rule="evenodd" /></svg>						</div>
											</div>


					<!-- Error Message (server validation errors only) -->
					<div x-show="birthdayError" x-transition class="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg">
						<p class="text-sm text-red-700" x-text="birthdayError"></p>
					</div>

					<button
						type="submit"
						x-show="!birthdayIsEditing || birthdayChanged"
						x-bind:class="birthdaySubmitting ? 'pointer-events-none' : ''"
						class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center"
					>
						<span
							class="[grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200"
							x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': birthdaySubmitting, 'visible': !birthdaySubmitting }"
							x-text="birthdayIsEditing ? 'Update birthday' : 'Set my birthday'"
						></span>
						<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: birthdaySubmitting !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: birthdaySubmitting === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>					</button>
				</form>
			</div>

		</div>
	</div>

			<div
		x-show="currentPanel === 'ways-to-redeem'"
		x-bind:class="'ways-to-redeem-panel relative h-full flex flex-col overflow-hidden ' + (currentPanel === 'ways-to-redeem' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
		x-data="{ panelAtTop: true, panelAtBottom: false }"
		x-effect="if (currentPanel === 'ways-to-redeem') { const el = $el.querySelector('.overflow-y-auto'); if (el && el.scrollHeight > 0) { panelAtBottom = el.scrollTop + el.clientHeight >= el.scrollHeight - 5; } }"
	>
		<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 py-6 pb-32 text-rwp-widget-text"
			@scroll="panelAtTop = $el.scrollTop < 5; panelAtBottom = $el.scrollTop + $el.clientHeight >= $el.scrollHeight - 5"
		>

			<h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-3">Ways to redeem</h2>

						<div class="divide-y divide-gray-50" x-data="redeemManager">
																<div class="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto] items-center gap-4 py-4" data-action-id="spend_1">
								<div class="size-8 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-rwp-widget-icon-text">
									<svg class="size-8" viewBox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
			<desc>
				Fixed amount discount icon
			</desc>
			<path d="M19.575 8.25a3.755 3.755 0 0 1 3.049 -2.95 0.749 0.749 0 0 0 0.626 -0.738V2.25a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 -1.5 -1.5H2.25a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 -1.5 1.5v2.314a0.747 0.747 0 0 0 0.626 0.738 3.751 3.751 0 0 1 0 7.4 0.75 0.75 0 0 0 -0.626 0.739v2.309a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 1.5 1.5h6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m8.25 14.25 0 -1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m8.25 9.75 0 -1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m8.25 5.25 0 -1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="M18.75 14.25h-2.033a1.342 1.342 0 0 0 -0.5 2.588l2.064 0.825a1.342 1.342 0 0 1 -0.5 2.587H15.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m17.25 14.25 0 -0.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m17.25 21 0 -0.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="M11.25 17.25a6 6 0 1 0 12 0 6 6 0 1 0 -12 0Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			</svg>								</div>
							<div class="min-w-0">
								<div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">$5 off coupon</div>
								<div class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">1,000 Points</div>

									<!-- Success Message -->
									<div x-show="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_1']?.success" x-transition class="text-sm text-green-600 mt-1">
										<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
											<svg class="size-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
												<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
											</svg>
											Coupon generated and applied to cart!										</div>
									</div>

									<!-- Error Message -->
									<div x-show="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_1']?.error" x-transition class="text-sm text-red-600 mt-1">
										<div class="flex items-center gap-2">

											<span x-text="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_1']?.errorMessage"></span>
										</div>
									</div>
								</div>

									<div class="justify-self-end" x-show="memberId" x-cloak >
					<button
													x-on:click="if (!isAdminPreview) { redeemSuccessData.redeemedLabel = '$5 off coupon'; redeemPointsForCoupon('fixed_discount', 'spend_1', 'fixed_discount_spend_1'); }"
							x-bind:disabled="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_1']?.redeeming || (userData?.points ?? 0) < 1000"
																		x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
						x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
						x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
												class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 py-2 px-4 grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center min-h-[36px] min-w-[5rem]"
					>
							<span
								class="[grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200"
																x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_1']?.redeeming, 'visible': !redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_1']?.redeeming }"
															>Redeem</span>
							<svg class="size-4 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: redeemStates[&#039;fixed_discount_spend_1&#039;]?.redeeming !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: redeemStates[&#039;fixed_discount_spend_1&#039;]?.redeeming === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>						</button>
					</div>

					</div>
																	<div class="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto] items-center gap-4 py-4" data-action-id="spend_2">
								<div class="size-8 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-rwp-widget-icon-text">
									<svg class="size-8" viewBox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
			<desc>
				Fixed amount discount icon
			</desc>
			<path d="M19.575 8.25a3.755 3.755 0 0 1 3.049 -2.95 0.749 0.749 0 0 0 0.626 -0.738V2.25a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 -1.5 -1.5H2.25a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 -1.5 1.5v2.314a0.747 0.747 0 0 0 0.626 0.738 3.751 3.751 0 0 1 0 7.4 0.75 0.75 0 0 0 -0.626 0.739v2.309a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 1.5 1.5h6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m8.25 14.25 0 -1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m8.25 9.75 0 -1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m8.25 5.25 0 -1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="M18.75 14.25h-2.033a1.342 1.342 0 0 0 -0.5 2.588l2.064 0.825a1.342 1.342 0 0 1 -0.5 2.587H15.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m17.25 14.25 0 -0.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>

							<div class="min-w-0">
								<div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">$10 off coupon</div>
								<div class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">2,000 Points</div>

									<!-- Success Message -->
									<div x-show="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_2']?.success" x-transition class="text-sm text-green-600 mt-1">
										<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
											<svg class="size-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
												<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
											</svg>
											Coupon generated and applied to cart!										</div>
									</div>

									<!-- Error Message -->
									<div x-show="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_2']?.error" x-transition class="text-sm text-red-600 mt-1">
										<div class="flex items-center gap-2">

											<span x-text="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_2']?.errorMessage"></span>
										</div>
									</div>
								</div>

									<div class="justify-self-end" x-show="memberId" x-cloak >
					<button
													x-on:click="if (!isAdminPreview) { redeemSuccessData.redeemedLabel = '$10 off coupon'; redeemPointsForCoupon('fixed_discount', 'spend_2', 'fixed_discount_spend_2'); }"
							x-bind:disabled="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_2']?.redeeming || (userData?.points ?? 0) < 2000"
																		x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
						x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
						x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
												class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 py-2 px-4 grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center min-h-[36px] min-w-[5rem]"
					>
							<span
								class="[grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200"
																x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_2']?.redeeming, 'visible': !redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_2']?.redeeming }"
															>Redeem</span>
							<svg class="size-4 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: redeemStates[&#039;fixed_discount_spend_2&#039;]?.redeeming !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: redeemStates[&#039;fixed_discount_spend_2&#039;]?.redeeming === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>						</button>
					</div>

					</div>
																	<div class="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto] items-center gap-4 py-4" data-action-id="spend_3">
								<div class="size-8 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-rwp-widget-icon-text">
									<svg class="size-8" viewBox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
			<desc>
				Fixed amount discount icon
			</desc>
			<path d="M19.575 8.25a3.755 3.755 0 0 1 3.049 -2.95 0.749 0.749 0 0 0 0.626 -0.738V2.25a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 -1.5 -1.5H2.25a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 -1.5 1.5v2.314a0.747 0.747 0 0 0 0.626 0.738 3.751 3.751 0 0 1 0 7.4 0.75 0.75 0 0 0 -0.626 0.739v2.309a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 1.5 1.5h6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>

			<path d="m17.25 14.25 0 -0.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m17.25 21 0 -0.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="M11.25 17.25a6 6 0 1 0 12 0 6 6 0 1 0 -12 0Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			</svg>								</div>
							<div class="min-w-0">
								<div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">$20 off coupon</div>
								<div class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">4,000 Points</div>

									<!-- Success Message -->
									<div x-show="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_3']?.success" x-transition class="text-sm text-green-600 mt-1">
										<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
											<svg class="size-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
												<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
											</svg>
											Coupon generated and applied to cart!										</div>
									</div>

									<!-- Error Message -->
									<div x-show="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_3']?.error" x-transition class="text-sm text-red-600 mt-1">
										<div class="flex items-center gap-2">

											<span x-text="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_3']?.errorMessage"></span>
										</div>
									</div>
								</div>

									<div class="justify-self-end" x-show="memberId" x-cloak >
					<button
													x-on:click="if (!isAdminPreview) { redeemSuccessData.redeemedLabel = '$20 off coupon'; redeemPointsForCoupon('fixed_discount', 'spend_3', 'fixed_discount_spend_3'); }"
							x-bind:disabled="redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_3']?.redeeming || (userData?.points ?? 0) < 4000"
																		x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
						x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
						x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
												class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 py-2 px-4 grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center min-h-[36px] min-w-[5rem]"
					>
							<span
								class="[grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200"
																x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_3']?.redeeming, 'visible': !redeemStates['fixed_discount_spend_3']?.redeeming }"
															>Redeem</span>
							<svg class="size-4 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: redeemStates[&#039;fixed_discount_spend_3&#039;]?.redeeming !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: redeemStates[&#039;fixed_discount_spend_3&#039;]?.redeeming === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>						</button>
					</div>

					</div>
									</div>
					</div>

		<!-- Top scroll fade -->
		<div x-show="!panelAtTop"
			class="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 h-8 z-10"
			style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>
		<!-- Bottom scroll fade — hidden when guest join section is visible -->
		<div x-show="!panelAtBottom && memberId !== 0"
			class="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-12 z-10"
			style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>

				<div x-show="memberId === 0">
		<!-- Fade Overlay -->
		<div
			class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-24 pointer-events-none"
			style="background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), var(--color-rwp-widget-bg) 20%, transparent);"
			x-data="{ hideJoinSection: false }"
			x-on:member-joined.window="hideJoinSection = true"
			x-show="!hideJoinSection"
		></div>

		<!-- Sticky Join Section -->
		<div
			class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-white px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-100"
			x-data="{ hideJoinSection: false, isLoggedIn: false }"
			x-on:member-joined.window="hideJoinSection = true"
			x-show="!hideJoinSection"
		>
	<div>
	<button
		type="button"
		x-on:click="window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-register-form'))"
		class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center"
	>
			Join now		</button>
		</div>
				<div x-show="!isLoggedIn" x-cloak class="mt-3 text-center text-rwp-widget-text">
					<p class="text-sm text-gray-600">
						Already have an account?						<button
							x-on:click="navigateToPanel('main', { resetScroll: true }); $dispatch('show-login-form');"
							class="font-medium underline text-rwp-accent cursor-pointer"
						>Sign in</button>
					</p>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
					</div>



				<div
			x-show="currentPanel === 'activity'"
			x-init="$watch('currentPanel', value => {
				if (value === 'activity') {
					if (activityItems.length === 0 || needsActivityRefresh) {
						if (needsActivityRefresh && activityTab === 'points') {
							activityItems = [];
							activityPage = 1;
							activityHasMore = false;
							activityCache = {};
						}
						loadActivity();
						needsActivityRefresh = false;
					}
				}
			})"
			x-bind:class="'relative h-full flex flex-col ' + (currentPanel === 'activity' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
		>
			<div class="shrink-0 px-6 pt-6 text-rwp-widget-text">
				<h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-3">Activity</h2>
				<!-- Segmented Control (hidden when only one tab is available) -->
				<div class="relative w-full mb-3" x-show="(pointsEnabled || (!!memberId && userData.points > 0)) && referralsEnabled">
					<div class="relative inline-grid items-center justify-center w-full h-10 grid-cols-2 p-1 text-gray-500 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl select-none" role="tablist">
						<button
							x-ref="activityPointsBtn"
							x-show="pointsEnabled || (!!memberId && userData.points > 0)"
							x-on:click="switchActivityTab('points')"
							type="button"
							class="relative z-20 inline-flex items-center justify-center w-full h-8 px-3 text-sm font-medium transition-all rounded-md cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
							x-bind:class="activityTab === 'points' ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-500'"
							role="tab"
							x-bind:aria-selected="activityTab === 'points'"
						>
							Points						</button>
						<button
							x-ref="activityReferralsBtn"
							x-show="referralsEnabled"
							x-on:click="switchActivityTab('referrals')"
							type="button"
							class="relative z-20 inline-flex items-center justify-center w-full h-8 px-3 text-sm font-medium transition-all rounded-md cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
							x-bind:class="activityTab === 'referrals' ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-500'"
							role="tab"
							x-bind:aria-selected="activityTab === 'referrals'"
						>
							Referrals						</button>
						<div class="absolute z-10 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm left-1 top-1 transition-transform duration-350 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.22,1.3,0.36,1)]" x-bind:style="'width: calc(50% - 4px); height: 32px; transform: translateX(' + ({'points': 0, 'referrals': 100}[activityTab] ?? 0) + '%)'"></div>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
			<div class="relative flex-1 min-h-0">
			<div x-ref="activityScroll" x-on:scroll.passive="syncActivityFade()" class="h-full overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 pt-1 pb-6 text-rwp-widget-text">

				<!-- Loading State -->
				<div x-show="activityLoading" class="flex items-center justify-center py-8">
					<div class="animate-spin size-6 border-2 border-gray-200 border-t-gray-600 rounded-full"></div>
				</div>

				<!-- Activity List -->
				<div x-show="!activityLoading">
					<!-- Empty State - Points Tab -->
					<template x-if="activityItems.length === 0 && !activityLoading && activityTab === 'points'">
						<div class="text-center py-8">
							<div class="flex justify-center mb-3">
								<svg class="size-10 text-gray-400" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
			<desc>
				Points activity icon
			</desc>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M12 2V1" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m12 23 0 -2.7661" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M1 12h2.71875" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M20.2812 12H23" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m6.0625 19.2258 -2.01636 2.0164" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m17.9375 5.03979 2.0164 -2.01635" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M6.0625 5.03979 4.04614 3.02344" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m17.9375 18.9602 2.0164 2.0164" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m12.4169 4.69626 1.9151 3.82921 3.6838 0.36877c0.0879 0.00861 0.1715 0.04201 0.2412 0.09631 0.0696 0.0543 0.1224 0.12727 0.1522 0.21041 0.0297 0.08314 0.0352 0.17302 0.0159 0.25918 -0.0193 0.08616 -0.0628 0.16505 -0.1252 0.22748L15.264 12.7225l1.1256 4.1238c0.0235 0.0869 0.0214 0.1787 -0.006 0.2644 -0.0275 0.0857 -0.0791 0.1616 -0.1486 0.2187 -0.0696 0.057 -0.1542 0.0928 -0.2436 0.1029 -0.0894 0.0101 -0.1798 -0.0058 -0.2604 -0.0458L12 15.5205l-3.73099 1.8679c-0.08052 0.0402 -0.17096 0.0562 -0.2604 0.0462 -0.08943 -0.0101 -0.17403 -0.0459 -0.24358 -0.103 -0.06955 -0.0571 -0.12107 -0.1332 -0.14833 -0.2189 -0.02727 -0.0858 -0.02911 -0.1776 -0.00531 -0.2644l1.1246 -4.1258 -3.03584 -3.03681c-0.06245 -0.06243 -0.10588 -0.14132 -0.12523 -0.22748 -0.01935 -0.08615 -0.01382 -0.17604 0.01595 -0.25918 0.02976 -0.08313 0.08254 -0.1561 0.15217 -0.21041 0.06964 -0.0543 0.15327 -0.0877 0.24115 -0.09631l3.68382 -0.36876 1.91509 -3.82922c0.0389 -0.07736 0.0985 -0.14238 0.1723 -0.18774 0.0737 -0.04536 0.1586 -0.06929 0.2452 -0.06909 0.0866 0.0002 0.1714 0.02452 0.2449 0.07022 0.0736 0.04571 0.1329 0.11099 0.1714 0.18854Z" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			</svg>							</div>
							<h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 mb-1" x-text="'No ' + currencyPlural + ' yet'"></h3>
							<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4" x-text="'See all the ways you can earn ' + currencyPlural"></p>
							<button
								x-on:click="navigateToPanel('ways-to-earn')"
								class="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 border border-transparent text-sm font-medium rounded-md text-white bg-rwp-brand hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover cursor-pointer transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-rwp-brand focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
							>
								<span x-text="'Earn ' + currencyPlural"></span>
							</button>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Empty State - Referrals Tab -->
					<template x-if="activityItems.length === 0 && !activityLoading && activityTab === 'referrals'">
						<div class="text-center py-8">
							<div class="flex justify-center mb-3">
								<svg class="size-10 text-gray-400" viewBox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
			<desc>
				Refer a friend icon
			</desc>
			<path d="m3.132 12.8 0.448 -1.175 -1.046 -2.744a0.929 0.929 0 0 1 0.36 -1.107l2.458 -1.605 0.767 -2.834a0.928 0.928 0 0 1 0.942 -0.684l2.932 0.149L12.279 0.955a0.928 0.928 0 0 1 1.165 0L15.73 2.8l2.932 -0.146a0.928 0.928 0 0 1 0.942 0.684l0.767 2.834 2.458 1.605a0.928 0.928 0 0 1 0.36 1.107l-1.045 2.744 1.045 2.743a0.929 0.929 0 0 1 -0.36 1.108l-2.458 1.6 -0.771 2.835a0.927 0.927 0 0 1 -0.942 0.685l-2.932 -0.146 -2.286 1.841" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m0.75 19.769 1.656 2.609" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m9.908 8.147 6.294 9.916" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="M15.871 17.541 2.075 21.857l-0.994 -1.566 9.158 -11.622 5.632 8.872z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m5.056 20.924 0.876 1.346a2.041 2.041 0 0 0 2.906 0.614 2.256 2.256 0 0 0 0.587 -3.04l-0.163 -0.236" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			</svg>							</div>
							<h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 mb-1">No referrals yet</h3>
							<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">When friends shop using your referral link, you&#039;ll see their activity here</p>
							<button
								x-on:click="navigateToPanel('main')"
								class="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 border border-transparent text-sm font-medium rounded-md text-white bg-rwp-brand hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover cursor-pointer transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-rwp-brand focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
							>
								Invite friends							</button>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Referral Summary Row (shown above activity items when referrals tab has data) -->
					<div x-show="activityTab === 'referrals' && activityItems.length > 0 && activityStatusCounts" class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-500 pb-2 mb-1">
						<span x-text="(activityStatusCounts ? activityStatusCounts.completed : 0) + ' completed'"></span>
						<span x-show="activityStatusCounts && !!activityStatusCounts.pending">&middot;</span>
						<span x-show="activityStatusCounts && !!activityStatusCounts.pending" x-text="(activityStatusCounts ? activityStatusCounts.pending : 0) + ' pending'"></span>
					</div>

					<!-- Activity Items -->
					<div class="divide-y divide-gray-100">
						<template x-for="item in activityItems" x-bind:key="item.id">
							<div class="py-3">
								<div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
									<!-- Left column: Description + Date -->
									<div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
										<!-- Referrals tab: Separate friend_name (semi-bold) and action_text (normal) -->
										<template x-if="activityTab === 'referrals' && item.friend_name">
											<p class="text-sm text-gray-900">
												<span class="font-semibold" x-text="item.friend_name"></span>
												<template x-if="item.action_text">
													<span class="font-normal" x-text="' ' + item.action_text"></span>
												</template>
											</p>
										</template>
										<!-- Points/other tabs: Primary line: Reward/action name -->
										<template x-if="activityTab !== 'referrals' || !item.friend_name">
											<p class="text-sm text-gray-900" x-text="item.target || item.action"></p>
										</template>
										<!-- Small text for order_id (refunds) -->
										<template x-if="item.order_id">
											<p class="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5" x-text="'#' + item.order_id"></p>
										</template>
										<!-- Small text for product_name (product reviews) -->
										<template x-if="item.product_name">
											<p class="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5" x-text="item.product_name"></p>
										</template>
										<!-- Secondary line: Date (all tabs) -->
										<p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5" x-text="item.date"></p>
									</div>
									<!-- Right column: Points value + currency OR Status pill -->
									<div class="flex-shrink-0 text-right">
										<!-- Points tab: Point value (with inline pending badge) + currency name below -->
										<template x-if="activityTab === 'points' && item.points !== undefined">
											<div>
												<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
													<p x-bind:class="item.source_type === 'points_expiration' ? 'text-gray-500' : (item.is_deduction ? 'text-gray-700' : (item.status === 'pending' ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-emerald-600'))"
														class="text-sm font-semibold"
														x-text="(item.is_deduction ? '-' : '+') + Math.abs(item.points).toLocaleString()">
													</p>
													<span x-show="item.status === 'pending'"
														class="text-[11px] text-gray-500">
														Pending													</span>
												</div>
												<p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5 text-right"
													x-text="getCurrencyName(Math.abs(item.points))">
												</p>
											</div>
										</template>
										<!-- Referrals/rewards tab: Status badge -->
										<template x-if="activityTab !== 'points' && item.status">
											<span
												x-bind:class="{
													'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-100': item.status === 'completed' || item.status === 'redeemed',
													'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-100': item.status === 'pending',
													'bg-gray-50 text-gray-700 border border-gray-200': item.status === 'issued',
													'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-100': item.status === 'blocked' || item.status === 'rejected',
													'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border border-gray-200': item.status === 'canceled'
												}"
												class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium mt-0.5"
												x-text="item.status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + item.status.slice(1)"
											></span>
										</template>
									</div>
								</div>
							</div>
						</template>
					</div>

					<!-- Load More -->
					<div x-show="activityHasMore" class="mt-4 text-center">
						<button
							x-on:click="loadMoreActivity()"
							x-bind:disabled="activityLoadingMore"
							class="text-sm text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 font-medium cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
						>
							<span x-show="!activityLoadingMore">Load more</span>
							<span x-show="activityLoadingMore" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
								<span class="animate-spin size-3.5 border-2 border-gray-200 border-t-gray-600 rounded-full"></span>
								Loading...							</span>
						</button>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
			<!-- Top scroll fade -->
			<div
				x-show="activityShowTopFade"
				class="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 h-8 z-10"
				style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"
			></div>
			<!-- Bottom scroll fade -->
			<div
				x-show="activityShowFade"
				class="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-12 rounded-b-2xl"
				style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"
			></div>
			</div>
		</div>

				<div
			x-show="currentPanel === 'create-account-prompt'"
			x-bind:class="'create-account-prompt-panel relative h-full flex flex-col ' + (currentPanel === 'create-account-prompt' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
		>
			<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 py-6 text-rwp-widget-text">
				<!-- Icon and Header -->
				<div class="text-center mb-6">

					<div class="relative inline-block mb-2">
						<!-- Main reward icon -->
						<div class="size-10 flex items-center justify-center text-rwp-widget-text/45" x-show="lockedReward" x-html="lockedReward?.iconHtml">
							<!-- Icon will be populated by Alpine -->
						</div>
					</div>
					<h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-3">
						<span x-show="lockedReward" x-text="lockedReward?.title"></span>
					</h2>
					<p class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text">
						Requires an account to redeem					</p>

				</div>

				<!-- Benefits List -->
				<div class="space-y-3 mb-8">
					<div class="flex items-start gap-3">
						<svg class="size-5 text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>						<div class="flex-1">
							<p class="text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-heading">
								Redeem rewards now							</p>
							<p class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text">
								Turn your <span x-text="currencyPlural.toLowerCase()"></span> into discounts and perks							</p>
						</div>
					</div>
					<div class="flex items-start gap-3">
						<svg class="size-5 text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>						<div class="flex-1">
							<p class="text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-heading">
								Instant setup							</p>
							<p class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text">
								One click and you&#039;re ready to go							</p>
						</div>
					</div>
											<div class="flex items-start gap-3">
							<svg class="size-5 text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>							<div class="flex-1">
								<p class="text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-heading">
									Earn even more								</p>
								<p class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text">
									Get +50 bonus <span x-text="currencyPlural.toLowerCase()"></span> for signing up								</p>
							</div>
						</div>
									</div>

		<!-- Create Account Button -->
		<button
			type="button"
			x-on:click="window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-register-form'))"
			class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center w-full"
		>
			Create account to use points		</button>

				<!-- Back Link -->
				<div class="text-center mt-4">
					<button
						type="button"
						x-on:click="navigateToPanel('ways-to-redeem')"
						class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text hover:text-rwp-widget-text underline cursor-pointer"
					>
						Back to rewards					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- Redeem Success Panel -->
		<div
			x-show="currentPanel === 'redeem-success'"
			x-bind:class="'redeem-success-panel relative h-full flex flex-col ' + (currentPanel === 'redeem-success' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
		>
			<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 py-6 pb-4 text-rwp-widget-text">
				<!-- Success Header -->
				<div
					class="text-center mb-6"
					x-data="{
						cartSubtotal: 0,
						currencySymbol: '$'
					}"
				>
					<div class="flex justify-center mb-2">
						<svg class="size-10 text-green-600" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><desc>Check circle alternate</desc><path d="M6 13.223 8.45 16.7a1.049 1.049 0 0 0 1.707 0.051L18 6.828" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M0.75 11.999a11.25 11.25 0 1 0 22.5 0 11.25 11.25 0 1 0 -22.5 0Z" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path></svg>					</div>
					<p class="text-base text-gray-600 mt-2">
						Redeemed 						<span x-text="redeemSuccessData.points_cost"></span>
						<span x-text="' ' + redeemSuccessData.points_label"></span>
					</p>
					<h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 text-pretty" x-text="redeemSuccessData.title">
						<!-- Title populated from Alpine data -->
					</h2>
					</div>

						<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
				<div class="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center mb-2 px-2">
					<p>Use this coupon on your next order</p>
				</div>
			</template>

						<div>
								<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
					<div class="mb-4" x-data="copyHandler()">
						<div x-data="{ isCheckmark: false }" class="flex rounded-lg bg-white outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200">
							<input
								type="text"
								readonly
								x-ref="couponCode"
								x-bind:value="redeemSuccessData.couponCode"
								x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, null, false)"
								class="block min-w-0 grow px-5 py-3 text-sm font-mono text-gray-900 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none sm:text-sm/6 text-ellipsis"
							>
							<div class="flex">
								<button
									x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data)"
									class="py-3 px-3 relative transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
									x-bind:class="{ 'hover:text-gray-900 text-gray-500': !isCheckmark}"
									x-ref="copyButton">
									<div class="relative size-6">
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
											x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-0 scale-95' : 'opacity-100 scale-100'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="-1 -1 24 24"><desc>Copy to clipboard icon</desc><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M17.875 6.645833333333333h2.520833333333333c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666V17.875" stroke-width="2"></path><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M0.6875 1.6041666666666665c0 -0.5062658333333333 0.41041 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666h12.833333333333332c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666v-12.833333333333332Z" stroke-width="2"></path></svg>										</div>
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
											x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-100 scale-100' : 'opacity-0 scale-95'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>										</div>
									</div>
								</button>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</template>

				<!-- Button and messages section -->
				<div>

				<!-- Cart-aware Button Section -->
				<div
					x-data="couponUtils( {
						nonce: '480d6bf443',
						buttonText: 'Apply to cart',
						rewardId: redeemSuccessData.reward_id || 0,
						couponCode: redeemSuccessData.couponCode || '',
						isApplied: redeemSuccessData.is_applied || false,
						cartItemCount: 0,
						cartProductIds: [],
						cartSubtotal: 0,
						freeProductId: redeemSuccessData.free_product_id || null,
						minimumOrderAmount: redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount || 0,
						integrationSource: 'woocommerce',
						checkoutUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/checkout/',
						cartUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/cart/',
						storeUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/shop/',
						isAdminPreview: false,
						isDemo: false,
						addToCartText: 'Add to cart',
						claimRewardText: 'Claim reward',
						applyToCartText: 'Claim free product',
						currencySymbol: '$',
						pendingRewardId: 0,
						dismissedRewardId: 0					} )"
					x-on:reset-buttons.window="if ( $event.detail.except !== $el ) isApplied = false;"
				>
									<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending && (redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount)">
						<p class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 text-center mb-2"></p>
					</template>

									<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending && redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount > 0 && cartSubtotal < redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount">
						<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 text-center text-pretty mb-2" x-text="'Claim now — we\'ll add it to your cart when you spend ' + currencySymbol + redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount + ' or more'"></p>
					</template>

									<button
						x-show="!isApplied && !isPending && !productAddedButCouponFailed"
						class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center"
						x-on:click="applyCoupon( redeemSuccessData.couponCode, redeemSuccessData.reward_id )"
						x-bind:disabled="isApplying"
						x-bind:class="{
							'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed': isApplying,
							'hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover': !isApplying
						}"
					>
						<span
							class="[grid-area:stack] flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity duration-200"
							x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': isApplying, 'visible': !isApplying }"
						>
							<!-- Dynamic text based on reward type, cart state, minimum spend, and integration -->
							<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product'">
								<span x-text="getFreeProductButtonText(redeemSuccessData.free_product_id, redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount)"></span>
							</template>
							<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
								<span>
							<span x-show="isWooCommerce">Apply to cart</span>
							<span x-show="!isWooCommerce">Apply coupon</span>
								</span>
							</template>
						</span>
							<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: isApplying !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: isApplying === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>						</button>

					<div x-show="hasErrors" class="text-sm mt-2 text-red-500">An error occurred. Please reload the page and try again.</div>

					<!-- Success Message - Show when coupon applied -->
					<div x-show="isApplied" x-cloak class="mb-1 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-emerald-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
						<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product'">
							<span>Free product added to cart</span>
						</template>
						<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
							<span x-text="redeemSuccessData.title + ' applied'"></span>
						</template>
					</div>

										<template x-if="isPending && !productAddedButCouponFailed">
						<div class="text-sm w-full">
							<!-- Confirmation badge -->
							<div class="font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
								<svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
									<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
								</svg>
								<span>Reward claimed</span>
							</div>

							<!-- Explanation text -->
							<div class="text-center text-gray-500 px-2 mb-3" x-text="'We\'ll add it to your cart when you spend ' + currencySymbol + redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount">
							</div>

							<!-- Action buttons -->
							<div class="space-y-3">
								<!-- Primary CTA - Start/Continue Shopping -->
								<a
									x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
									x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
