									x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
									x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
									x-text="isCartEmpty ? 'Start shopping' : 'Continue shopping'"
								></a>

								<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only, when cart has items) -->
								<template x-if="isWooCommerce && !isCartEmpty">
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
										class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
									>
										View cart									</a>
								</template>
							</div>
						</div>
					</template>

										<div x-show="productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending" x-cloak class="mt-3 mb-3 text-sm w-full space-y-2">
						<div x-show="productWasNewlyAdded" class="text-center text-emerald-600 font-medium">
							Product added to cart						</div>
												<template x-if="remainingToUnlock > 0">
							<div class="text-center text-gray-600 px-2" x-text="'Almost there — add $' + remainingToUnlock + ' more to unlock'"></div>
						</template>
					</div>

					<!-- Buttons when coupon failed but product added (fallback) -->
					<template x-if="productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending">
						<div>
							<!-- Primary CTA - Continue Shopping -->
							<a
								x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
								x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
								x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
								x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
								class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
							>
								Continue shopping							</a>

							<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
							<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
								<a
									x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
									x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
									x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
									x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
								>
									View cart								</a>
							</template>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Error Message - Show when coupon failed to apply (no product involved) -->
					<div x-show="hasErrors && !productAddedButCouponFailed" x-cloak class="mb-1 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-red-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
						<span x-text="errorMessage"></span>
					</div>

					<!-- Buttons when coupon is applied successfully -->
					<template x-if="isApplied && !isCartEmpty">
						<div>
							<!-- Primary CTA - Go to Checkout -->
							<a
								x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : checkoutUrl"
								x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
								x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
								class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
								x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
							>
								Go to checkout							</a>

							<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
							<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
								<a
									x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
									x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
									x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
									x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
								>
									View cart								</a>
							</template>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Button when coupon is applied but cart is empty -->
					<template x-if="isApplied && isCartEmpty">
						<div>
							<!-- Primary CTA - Shop Now -->
							<a
								x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
								x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
								x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
								x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
								class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
							>
								Shop now							</a>
							<!-- No secondary button when cart is empty -->
						</div>
					</template>

				<!-- Coupon Terms (below buttons) - show only expiry when applied or ready (minimum met). -->
				<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.coupon_terms">
					<div class="mt-2 text-center">
						<span class="text-sm text-gray-500" x-text="(isApplied || (redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isPending && (redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount))) && redeemSuccessData.expiry_text ? redeemSuccessData.expiry_text : redeemSuccessData.coupon_terms"></span>
					</div>
				</template>
				</div>
				</div>

			</div>

			</div>

						<div
				x-data="{
					couponFailed: false,
					showCode: false,
					isCheckmark: false,
					isPending: false,
					isApplied: false,
					cartSubtotal: 0,
					minimumOrderAmount: 0,
					pendingRewardId: 0,
					...copyHandler()
				}"
				x-init="
					minimumOrderAmount = redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount || 0;
					isPending = pendingRewardId > 0 && pendingRewardId === parseInt(redeemSuccessData.reward_id || 0, 10);
					window.addEventListener('free-product-coupon-failed', () => { couponFailed = true; });
					window.addEventListener('show-redeem-success', () => { couponFailed = false; isPending = false; isApplied = false; });
					window.addEventListener('reward-set-pending', () => { isPending = true; });
					window.addEventListener('reward-auto-applied', () => { isPending = false; isApplied = true; });
					window.addEventListener('coupon-applied', () => { isApplied = true; });
				"
				x-show="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && (isPending || (redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount))"
				x-cloak
				class="px-6 text-center bg-gray-50 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-col-reverse items-center"
			>
					<button
						x-on:click="if (!showCode) { showCode = true; $nextTick(() => { handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data); }); } else { showCode = false; }"
						class="text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-600 underline underline-offset-2 cursor-pointer bg-transparent border-0 my-4"
						x-text="showCode ? 'Hide code' : 'Use a code at checkout'"
					></button>

					<!-- Collapsible code section -->
					<div x-show="showCode" x-collapse x-cloak class="w-full">
						<div class="pt-4">
						<div class="flex rounded-lg bg-white outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200">
							<input
								type="text"
								readonly
								x-ref="couponCode"
								x-bind:value="redeemSuccessData.couponCode"
								x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, null, false)"
								class="block min-w-0 grow px-5 py-3 text-sm font-mono text-gray-900 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none sm:text-sm/6 text-ellipsis"
							>
							<div class="flex">
								<button
									x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data)"
									class="py-3 px-3 relative transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
									x-bind:class="{ 'hover:text-gray-900 text-gray-500': !isCheckmark}"
									x-ref="copyButton">
									<div class="relative size-6">
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
											x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-0 scale-95' : 'opacity-100 scale-100'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="-1 -1 24 24"><desc>Copy to clipboard icon</desc><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M17.875 6.645833333333333h2.520833333333333c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666V17.875" stroke-width="2"></path><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M0.6875 1.6041666666666665c0 -0.5062658333333333 0.41041 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666h12.833333333333332c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666v-12.833333333333332Z" stroke-width="2"></path></svg>										</div>
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
											x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-100 scale-100' : 'opacity-0 scale-95'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>										</div>
									</div>
								</button>
							</div>
						</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>


				<div
			x-show="currentPanel === 'your-rewards'"
			x-bind:class="'relative h-full flex flex-col overflow-hidden ' + (currentPanel === 'your-rewards' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
			x-data="{ panelAtTop: true, panelAtBottom: false }"
						x-effect="if (currentPanel === 'your-rewards') { void availableRewards?.length; const el = $el.querySelector('.overflow-y-auto'); if (el && el.scrollHeight > 0) { panelAtBottom = el.scrollTop + el.clientHeight >= el.scrollHeight - 5; } }"
		>
			<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-0 py-6"
				@scroll="panelAtTop = $el.scrollTop < 5; panelAtBottom = $el.scrollTop + $el.clientHeight >= $el.scrollHeight - 5"
			>

			<h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-3 px-6">Your rewards</h2>

			<div class="px-3">
				<template x-if="availableRewards && availableRewards.length > 0">
					<template x-for="reward in availableRewards" x-bind:key="reward.id">
						<div>
							<button
								x-on:click="navigateToPanel('reward-detail-' + reward.id)"
								class="w-full hover:bg-gray-50 text-left px-4 py-3 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 !px-3"
									>
							<div class="flex items-center gap-4">
								<div class="size-8 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-rwp-widget-icon-text" x-html="reward.icon_html">
									<!-- Icon will be populated from server -->
								</div>
								<div class="flex-1">
									<div class="font-medium text-rwp-widget-heading text-sm" x-text="reward.title"></div>
									<div class="text-xs text-gray-500">
										<template x-if="reward.is_out_of_stock">
											<span class="text-amber-600">Out of stock</span>
										</template>
										<template x-if="!reward.is_out_of_stock && reward.points_cost > 0">
											<span x-text="'Redeemed ' + reward.points_cost + ' ' + reward.points_label"></span>
										</template>
										<template x-if="!reward.is_out_of_stock && reward.points_cost === 0">
											<span>From referral</span>
										</template>
									</div>
								</div>
							</div>
										<svg class="size-5 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5">
											<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M8.25 4.5l7.5 7.5-7.5 7.5" />
								</svg>
								</button>
							</div>
						</template>
				</template>

				<!-- Empty state when no rewards -->
					<template x-if="!availableRewards || availableRewards.length === 0">
						<div class="text-center py-8">
							<div class="flex justify-center mb-3">
								<svg class="size-10 text-gray-400" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><desc>Free product icon</desc><g><g><path d="M15.75 23.24a3 3 0 0 0 -3 -3H9a3 3 0 0 0 -3 -3H0.75v6Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="m6 20.24 3 0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path></g><path d="M16.5 17.74H21a0.76 0.76 0 0 0 0.75 -0.75v-6.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="m8.25 10.24 0 4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M7.5 5.99h15s0.75 0 0.75 0.75v2.75s0 0.75 -0.75 0.75h-15s-0.75 0 -0.75 -0.75V6.74s0 -0.75 0.75 -0.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="m15 15.24 0 -9.25" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M19.27 3.87A11.84 11.84 0 0 1 15 6a11.93 11.93 0 0 1 2.12 -4.24C18.46 0.43 19.21 0.63 19.8 1.22s0.78 1.33 -0.53 2.65Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M10.73 3.87A11.84 11.84 0 0 0 15 6a11.93 11.93 0 0 0 -2.12 -4.24C11.54 0.43 10.79 0.63 10.2 1.22s-0.78 1.33 0.53 2.65Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path></g></svg>							</div>
							<h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 mb-1">No rewards yet</h3>
							<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-5">Start earning to unlock your first reward</p>
							<div class="flex justify-center">
								<button
									x-on:click="navigateToPanel('ways-to-earn')"
									class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 py-2 px-4 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-rwp-button-hover"
								>
									Earn Points								</button>
							</div>
						</div>
					</template>
				</div>
			</div>

			<!-- Top scroll fade -->
			<div x-show="!panelAtTop"
				class="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 h-8 z-10"
				style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>
			<!-- Bottom scroll fade -->
			<div x-show="!panelAtBottom"
				class="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-12 z-10"
				style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>
		</div>

				<template x-for="reward in availableRewards" x-bind:key="'detail-' + reward.id">
			<div
				x-show="currentPanel === 'reward-detail-' + reward.id"
				x-bind:class="'reward-detail-panel relative h-full flex flex-col ' + (currentPanel === 'reward-detail-' + reward.id ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
			>




				<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 py-6 pb-6 text-rwp-widget-text">
					<!-- Icon and Title (Centered like friend claim) -->
					<div
						class="text-center mb-6"
						x-data="{
							cartSubtotal: 0,
							currencySymbol: '$'
						}"
					>
						<div class="flex justify-center mx-auto mb-2 [&>svg]:size-10" x-html="reward.icon_html">
							<!-- Icon will be populated from server -->
						</div>
						<h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 text-pretty" x-text="reward.title"></h2>
					</div>

										<template x-if="reward.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
						<p class="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center mb-2" x-text="reward.description"></p>
					</template>

			<!-- Coupon Code Display with Copy Functionality -->
			<template x-if="reward.coupon_code && reward.coupon_code !== ''">
					<div>
												<template x-if="reward.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
							<div class="mb-4" x-data="copyHandler()">
								<div x-data="{ isCheckmark: false }" class="flex rounded-lg bg-white outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200">
									<input
										type="text"
										readonly
										x-ref="couponCode"
										x-bind:value="reward.coupon_code"
										x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, null, false)"
										class="block min-w-0 grow px-5 py-3 text-sm font-mono text-gray-900 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none sm:text-sm/6 text-ellipsis"
									>
									<div class="flex">
										<button
											x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data)"
											class="py-3 px-3 relative transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
											x-bind:class="{ 'hover:text-gray-900 text-gray-500': !isCheckmark}"
											x-ref="copyButton">
											<div class="relative size-6">
												<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
													x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-0 scale-95' : 'opacity-100 scale-100'">
													<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="-1 -1 24 24"><desc>Copy to clipboard icon</desc><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M17.875 6.645833333333333h2.520833333333333c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666V17.875" stroke-width="2"></path><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M0.6875 1.6041666666666665c0 -0.5062658333333333 0.41041 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666h12.833333333333332c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666v-12.833333333333332Z" stroke-width="2"></path></svg>												</div>
												<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
													x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-100 scale-100' : 'opacity-0 scale-95'">
													<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>												</div>
											</div>
										</button>
									</div>
								</div>
							</div>
						</template>

						<!-- Button and messages section -->
						<div>

					<!-- Out of Stock Notice -->
					<template x-if="reward.is_out_of_stock">
						<div class="text-center mb-4 p-4 bg-amber-50 rounded-lg">
							<p class="text-sm text-amber-700 font-medium">
								This product is currently out of stock							</p>
							<p class="text-xs text-amber-600 mt-1">
								Your reward will remain available when the product is back in stock.							</p>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Cart-aware Button Section -->
					<div
						x-data="couponUtils( {
							nonce: '480d6bf443',
							buttonText: 'Apply to cart',
							rewardId: reward.id,
							couponCode: reward.coupon_code || '',
							appliedButtonText: 'Coupon applied',
							isApplied: reward.is_applied || false,
							isOutOfStock: reward.is_out_of_stock || false,
							cartItemCount: 0,
							cartProductIds: [],
							cartSubtotal: 0,
							freeProductId: reward.free_product_id || null,
							minimumOrderAmount: reward.minimum_order_amount || 0,
							integrationSource: 'woocommerce',
							checkoutUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/checkout/',
							cartUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/cart/',
							storeUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/shop/',
							isAdminPreview: false,
							isDemo: false,
							addToCartText: 'Add to cart',
							claimRewardText: 'Claim reward',
							applyToCartText: 'Claim free product',
							currencySymbol: '$',
							pendingRewardId: 0,
							dismissedRewardId: 0						} )"
						x-on:reset-buttons.window="if ( $event.detail.except !== $el ) isApplied = false;"
					>
												<template x-if="reward.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending && (reward.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= reward.minimum_order_amount)">
							<p class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 text-center mb-2"></p>
						</template>

											<template x-if="reward.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending && reward.minimum_order_amount > 0 && cartSubtotal < reward.minimum_order_amount">
							<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 text-center text-pretty mb-2" x-text="'Claim now — we\'ll add it to your cart when you spend ' + currencySymbol + reward.minimum_order_amount + ' or more'"></p>
						</template>

											<button
							x-show="!isApplied && !isPending && !isOutOfStock && !productAddedButCouponFailed"
							class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center"
							x-on:click="applyCoupon( reward.coupon_code )"
							x-bind:disabled="isApplying"
							x-bind:class="{
								'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed': isApplying,
								'hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover': !isApplying
							}"
						>
							<span
								class="[grid-area:stack] flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity duration-200"
								x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': isApplying, 'visible': !isApplying }"
							>
								<!-- Dynamic text based on reward type, cart state, minimum spend, and integration -->
								<template x-if="reward.reward_type === 'free_product'">
									<span x-text="getFreeProductButtonText(reward.free_product_id, reward.minimum_order_amount)"></span>
								</template>
								<template x-if="reward.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
									<span>
								<span x-show="isWooCommerce">Apply to cart</span>
								<span x-show="!isWooCommerce">Apply coupon</span>
									</span>
								</template>
							</span>
							<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: isApplying !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: isApplying === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>						</button>

						<div x-show="hasErrors" class="text-sm mt-2 text-red-500">An error occurred. Please reload the page and try again.</div>

						<!-- Success Message - Show when coupon applied -->
						<div x-show="isApplied" x-cloak class="mb-1 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-emerald-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
							<template x-if="reward.reward_type === 'free_product'">
								<span>Free product added to cart</span>
							</template>
							<template x-if="reward.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
								<span x-text="reward.title + ' applied'"></span>
							</template>
						</div>

												<template x-if="isPending && !productAddedButCouponFailed">
							<div class="text-sm w-full">
								<!-- Confirmation badge -->
								<div class="font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
									<svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
										<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
									</svg>
									<span>Reward claimed</span>
								</div>

								<!-- Explanation text -->
								<div class="text-center text-gray-500 px-2 mb-3" x-text="'We\'ll add it to your cart when you spend ' + currencySymbol + reward.minimum_order_amount">
								</div>

								<!-- Action buttons -->
								<div class="space-y-3">
									<!-- Primary CTA - Start/Continue Shopping -->
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
										class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
										x-text="isCartEmpty ? 'Start shopping' : 'Continue shopping'"
									></a>

									<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only, when cart has items) -->
									<template x-if="isWooCommerce && !isCartEmpty">
										<a
											x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
											x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
											x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
											x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
											class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
										>
											View cart										</a>
									</template>
								</div>
							</div>
						</template>

						<!-- Partial Success Message - Product added but coupon failed (fallback for edge cases) -->
						<div x-show="productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending" x-cloak class="mt-3 mb-3 text-sm w-full space-y-2">
							<div x-show="productWasNewlyAdded" class="text-center text-emerald-600 font-medium">
								Product added to cart							</div>
														<template x-if="remainingToUnlock > 0">
								<div class="text-center text-gray-600 px-2" x-text="'Almost there — add $' + remainingToUnlock + ' more to unlock'"></div>
							</template>
						</div>

						<!-- Buttons when coupon failed but product added (fallback) -->
						<template x-if="productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending">
							<div>
								<!-- Primary CTA - Continue Shopping -->
								<a
									x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
									x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
									x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
									x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
								>
									Continue shopping								</a>

								<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
								<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
										class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
									>
										View cart									</a>
								</template>
							</div>
						</template>

						<!-- Error Message - Show when coupon failed to apply (no product involved) -->
						<div x-show="hasErrors && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending" x-cloak class="mb-1 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-red-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
							<span x-text="errorMessage"></span>
						</div>

							<!-- Buttons when coupon is applied successfully -->
							<template x-if="isApplied && !isCartEmpty">
								<div>
									<!-- Primary CTA - Go to Checkout -->
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : checkoutUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
										x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									>
										Go to checkout									</a>

									<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
									<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
										<a
											x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
											x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
											x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
											x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
											class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
										>
											View cart										</a>
									</template>
								</div>
							</template>

							<!-- Button when coupon is applied but cart is empty -->
							<template x-if="isApplied && isCartEmpty">
								<div>
									<!-- Primary CTA - Shop Now -->
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
										class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
									>
										Shop now									</a>
									<!-- No secondary button when cart is empty -->
								</div>
							</template>

						<!-- Coupon Terms (below buttons) - show only expiry when applied or ready (minimum met). -->
						<template x-if="reward.coupon_terms">
							<div class="mt-2 text-center">
								<span class="text-sm text-gray-500" x-text="(isApplied || (reward.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isPending && (reward.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= reward.minimum_order_amount))) && reward.expiry_text ? reward.expiry_text : reward.coupon_terms"></span>
							</div>
						</template>
						</div>
						</div>

					</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Back to Your Rewards Button -->



				</div>

								<div
					x-data="{
						couponFailed: false,
						showCode: false,
						isCheckmark: false,
						isPending: false,
						isApplied: false,
						cartSubtotal: 0,
						minimumOrderAmount: 0,
						pendingRewardId: 0,
						...copyHandler()
					}"
					x-init="
						minimumOrderAmount = reward.minimum_order_amount || 0;
						isApplied = reward.is_applied || false;
						isPending = pendingRewardId > 0 && pendingRewardId === parseInt(reward.id || 0, 10);
						window.addEventListener('free-product-coupon-failed', () => { couponFailed = true; });
						window.addEventListener('show-redeem-success', () => { couponFailed = false; isPending = false; });
						window.addEventListener('reward-set-pending', () => { isPending = true; });
						window.addEventListener('reward-auto-applied', () => { isPending = false; isApplied = true; });
						window.addEventListener('coupon-applied', () => { isApplied = true; });
					"
					x-show="reward.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && (isPending || (reward.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= reward.minimum_order_amount))"
					x-cloak
					class="px-6 text-center bg-gray-50 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-col-reverse items-center"
				>
					<button
						x-on:click="if (!showCode) { showCode = true; $nextTick(() => { handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data); }); } else { showCode = false; }"
							class="text-sm text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 underline underline-offset-2 cursor-pointer bg-transparent border-0 my-4"
							x-text="showCode ? 'Hide code' : 'Use a code at checkout'"
						></button>

						<!-- Collapsible code section -->
						<div x-show="showCode" x-collapse x-cloak class="w-full">
							<div class="pt-4">
							<div class="flex rounded-lg bg-white outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200">
								<input
									type="text"
									readonly
									x-ref="couponCode"
									x-bind:value="reward.coupon_code"
									x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, null, false)"
									class="block min-w-0 grow px-5 py-3 text-sm font-mono text-gray-900 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none sm:text-sm/6 text-ellipsis"
								>
								<div class="flex">
									<button
										x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data)"
										class="py-3 px-3 relative transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
										x-bind:class="{ 'hover:text-gray-900 text-gray-500': !isCheckmark}"
										x-ref="copyButton">
										<div class="relative size-6">
											<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
												x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-0 scale-95' : 'opacity-100 scale-100'">
												<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="-1 -1 24 24"><desc>Copy to clipboard icon</desc><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M17.875 6.645833333333333h2.520833333333333c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666V17.875" stroke-width="2"></path><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M0.6875 1.6041666666666665c0 -0.5062658333333333 0.41041 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666h12.833333333333332c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666v-12.833333333333332Z" stroke-width="2"></path></svg>											</div>
											<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
												x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-100 scale-100' : 'opacity-0 scale-95'">
												<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>											</div>
										</div>
									</button>
								</div>
							</div>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</template>

					</div>

						<div x-show="currentPanel === 'main'"
				x-bind:class="isScrolledToBottom ? 'opacity-0' : 'opacity-100'"
				class="sticky bottom-0 h-8 -mt-8 shrink-0 pointer-events-none z-40 transition-opacity duration-200"
				style="margin-right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>

		</div>

					</div>
		</template>

				<template x-if="!isMobile">
			<div
				id="rwp-rewards-panel"
				x-ref="rewardsPanel"
				x-bind:hidden="!open"
				class="rwp-desktop-panel rwp-desktop-panel--scale z-[var(--rwp-z-panel)] bg-rwp-widget-bg rounded-xl overflow-hidden"
				x-bind:data-panel-state="isFriendClaimMode ? 'friend-claim' : 'combined'"
				data-highlight="panel-bg"
				role="dialog"
				aria-labelledby="rewards-title"
			>
				





		



				<button
			x-show="!isFriendClaimMode"
			x-on:click="open = false"
			x-bind:class="'group p-2 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer z-[var(--rwp-z-close-button)] focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 ' + (isFriendClaimMode ? 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700' : 'text-rwp-widget-header-text') + ' ' + (isMobile ? 'fixed' : 'absolute top-[12px] right-[12px]')"
			x-bind:style="isMobile ? 'top: calc(env(safe-area-inset-top) + 12px); right: calc(env(safe-area-inset-right) + 12px);' : ''"
			aria-label="Close"
		>
			<div x-bind:class="'p-1 rounded transition-colors ' + (isFriendClaimMode ? 'group-hover:bg-gray-100' : 'group-hover:bg-white/10')">
				<div class="size-5 flex items-center justify-center">
					<svg class="size-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5"><desc>X (close) icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" /></svg>				</div>
			</div>
		</button>

				<div
			x-bind:hidden="!(currentPanel === 'main' && showFixedHeader && !isFriendClaimMode)"
			x-bind:class="'rwp-fixed-header absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-header-text flex items-center justify-between ' + (isMobile ? '' : 'px-5 h-16')"
			x-bind:style="isMobile ? 'padding-left: calc(env(safe-area-inset-left) + 20px); padding-right: calc(env(safe-area-inset-right) + 20px); padding-top: env(safe-area-inset-top); min-height: 64px;' : ''"
			data-highlight="header-bg"
		>
					<div class="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1 min-w-0">
						<button x-show="currentPanel !== 'main'" x-on:click="
					currentPanel === 'redeem-success' ? navigateToPanel('ways-to-redeem') :
					currentPanel === 'create-account-prompt' ? navigateToPanel('ways-to-redeem') :
					currentPanel.startsWith('reward-detail-') ? navigateToPanel('your-rewards') :
					currentPanel === 'birthday-picker' ? navigateToPanel('ways-to-earn') :
					navigateToPanel('main')
				" class="group p-2 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer -ml-2 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500" aria-label="Back">
				<div class="p-1 rounded transition-colors group-hover:bg-white/10">
					<div class="size-5 flex items-center justify-center">
						<svg class="size-7" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Chevron left icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M15.75 19.5 8.25 12l7.5-7.5" /></svg>					</div>
				</div>
			</button>
			<div>
				<div class="font-semibold text-base">
					<!-- Points balance (shown on all panels when points program is active) -->
					<span x-show="showPoints &amp;&amp; !!memberId"><span x-text="userData.points.toLocaleString()"></span> <span x-text="(userData.points == 1 ? currencySingular : currencyPlural)"></span></span>
					<!-- Referral count (shown on all panels when ONLY referrals program is active) -->
					<span x-show="showReferrals &amp;&amp; !showPoints &amp;&amp; !!memberId" x-text="friendsReferred === 0 ? 'No friends referred yet' : (friendsReferred === 1 ? '1 friend referred' : friendsReferred + ' friends referred')">
					</span>
					<!-- Fallback: Activity label when no member or no programs -->
					<span x-show="!memberId &amp;&amp; currentPanel === 'activity'">Activity</span>
				</div>
				<div class="text-sm">
					<span x-show="!isFriendClaimMode" x-text="programName"></span>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
				</div>

				<div
			x-show="currentPanel !== 'main' && !isFriendClaimMode"
			x-bind:class="'absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-header-text flex items-center justify-between ' + (isMobile ? '' : 'px-5 h-16')"
			x-bind:style="isMobile ? 'padding-left: calc(env(safe-area-inset-left) + 20px); padding-right: calc(env(safe-area-inset-right) + 20px); padding-top: env(safe-area-inset-top); min-height: 64px;' : ''"
			data-highlight="header-bg"
		>
					<div class="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1 min-w-0">
						<button x-show="currentPanel !== 'main'" x-on:click="
					currentPanel === 'redeem-success' ? navigateToPanel('ways-to-redeem') :
					currentPanel === 'create-account-prompt' ? navigateToPanel('ways-to-redeem') :
					currentPanel.startsWith('reward-detail-') ? navigateToPanel('your-rewards') :
					currentPanel === 'birthday-picker' ? navigateToPanel('ways-to-earn') :
					navigateToPanel('main')
				" class="group p-2 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer -ml-2 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500" aria-label="Back">
				<div class="p-1 rounded transition-colors group-hover:bg-white/10">
					<div class="size-5 flex items-center justify-center">
						<svg class="size-7" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Chevron left icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M15.75 19.5 8.25 12l7.5-7.5" /></svg>					</div>
				</div>
			</button>
			<div>
				<div class="font-semibold text-base">
					<!-- Points balance (shown on all panels when points program is active) -->
					<span x-show="showPoints &amp;&amp; !!memberId"><span x-text="userData.points.toLocaleString()"></span> <span x-text="(userData.points == 1 ? currencySingular : currencyPlural)"></span></span>
					<!-- Referral count (shown on all panels when ONLY referrals program is active) -->
					<span x-show="showReferrals &amp;&amp; !showPoints &amp;&amp; !!memberId" x-text="friendsReferred === 0 ? 'No friends referred yet' : (friendsReferred === 1 ? '1 friend referred' : friendsReferred + ' friends referred')">
					</span>
					<!-- Fallback: Activity label when no member or no programs -->
					<span x-show="!memberId &amp;&amp; currentPanel === 'activity'">Activity</span>
				</div>
				<div class="text-sm">
					<span x-show="!isFriendClaimMode" x-text="programName"></span>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
				</div>


				<template x-if="currentPanel === 'main' && !isFriendClaimMode">
			<div
				x-bind:class="'absolute top-0 left-0 w-full -z-10 bg-cover bg-center ' + (!(backgroundImage.enabled && backgroundImage.url && isProFeatureAccessible('pro')) ? ' bg-rwp-brand' : '')"
				x-bind:style="
				'transform: translate3d(0px, ' + getParallaxOffset(scrollTop) + 'px, 0px); ' +
				(backgroundImage.enabled && backgroundImage.url && isProFeatureAccessible('pro') ? 'background-image: url(' + backgroundImage.url + ');' : '') +
				'' +
				'height: ' + dynamicBackgroundHeight + 'px;'"
			>
			</div>
		</template>



		

		<div
			x-bind:class="'relative w-full h-full flex flex-col panel-container flex-1' + (currentPanel === 'main' ? ' overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden custom-scrollbar' : '')"
			@scroll="handleScroll($event)"
			x-ref="scrollContainer"
		>


						<div
				x-show="currentPanel === 'main' && !isFriendClaimMode"
				class="panel-expanded-header text-rwp-widget-header-text bg-cover bg-center relative"
			>
				<div class="banner-content px-5 pb-6 pt-16 h-full flex flex-col justify-end relative"
					x-ref="bannerContent"
					x-bind:style="'opacity: ' + getTextOpacity(scrollTop)"
				>
					<div class="relative z-10">
						<div x-show="memberId > 0">
														<template x-if="showPoints">
								<div>
									<div class="text-base" x-text="yourLabel + ' ' + (userData.points == 1 ? currencySingular : currencyPlural)"></div>
									<div class="font-bold text-4xl mb-1" x-text="userData.points.toLocaleString()"></div>
								</div>
							</template>

														<template x-if="showReferrals && !showPoints">
								<div>
									<div class="text-base">Share &amp; Earn with</div>
									<div class="font-semibold text-3xl" x-text="programName"></div>
								</div>
							</template>
						</div>

												<div x-show="memberId === 0">
							<div class="text-base">
								<span x-show="!isFriendClaimMode" x-text="welcomeGreeting"></span>
							</div>
							<div class="font-semibold text-3xl">
								<span x-show="!isFriendClaimMode" x-text="programName"></span>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>



						<div
				class="flex-1"
				x-bind:style="'height: 100%;' + (currentPanel !== 'main' ? ' padding-top: 64px;' : ' padding-top: 0;')"
				x-ref="contentArea"
			>
				

	<div
		x-show="!isFriendClaimMode && currentPanel === 'main'"
		x-bind:class="'px-4 pb-10 flex flex-col gap-4 ' + (currentPanel === 'main' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
	>

		<!-- Birthday banner (shows all day on member's birthday) -->
		<div
			x-show="birthdayBanner && memberId > 0"
			data-birthday-banner
			class="bg-white rounded-lg px-4 py-6 relative text-rwp-widget-text shadow-card px-5"
		>
			<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
				<div class="flex-shrink-0">
					<svg class="size-8 text-rwp-widget-icon-text" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
			<desc>Birthday cake icon</desc>
			<path d="M3.08108 11.7027H20.9189C20.9189 11.7027 22.7027 11.7027 22.7027 13.4865V21.2162C22.7027 21.2162 22.7027 23 20.9189 23H3.08108C3.08108 23 1.2973 23 1.2973 21.2162V13.4865C1.2973 13.4865 1.2973 11.7027 3.08108 11.7027Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>

			<path d="M1.2973 17.0184C1.57132 17.4845 1.96234 17.8709 2.43166 18.1394C2.90097 18.4079 3.43228 18.5492 3.97297 18.5492C4.51367 18.5492 5.04498 18.4079 5.51429 18.1394C5.9836 17.8709 6.37463 17.4845 6.64865 17.0184C6.92267 17.4845 7.3137 17.8709 7.78301 18.1394C8.25232 18.4079 8.78363 18.5492 9.32432 18.5492C9.86502 18.5492 10.3963 18.4079 10.8656 18.1394C11.335 17.8709 11.726 17.4845 12 17.0184C12.274 17.4845 12.665 17.8709 13.1344 18.1394C13.6037 18.4079 14.135 18.5492 14.6757 18.5492C15.2164 18.5492 15.7477 18.4079 16.217 18.1394C16.6863 17.8709 17.0773 17.4845 17.3514 17.0184C17.6254 17.4845 18.0164 17.8709 18.4857 18.1394C18.955 18.4079 19.4863 18.5492 20.027 18.5492C20.5677 18.5492 21.099 18.4079 21.5683 18.1394C22.0377 17.8709 22.4287 17.4845 22.7027 17.0184" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
			</svg>				</div>
				<div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
					<div class="text-base font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading">
						<span x-text="birthdayBanner ? 'Happy Birthday ' + birthdayBanner.member_name + '!' : ''"></span>
					</div>
					<div class="text-sm text-gray-500">
						<span x-text="birthdayBanner ? 'Enjoy ' + birthdayBanner.points_earned.toLocaleString() + ' ' + currencyPlural + ' on us' : ''"></span>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		

				<div x-show="memberId === 0 || isGuestMember">
	<div
		x-data="becomeMember({
			nonce: '933d3f6d66',
			ajaxUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php',
			isLoggedIn: false,
			isGuestMember: false,
			userName: '',
			userEmail: ''
		})"
		x-on:show-register-form.window="currentScreen = 'register'; showForm = true; showError = false; $dispatch('navigate-to-panel', { panel: 'main', resetScroll: true });"
		x-on:show-login-form.window="currentScreen = 'login'; showForm = true; showError = false;"
			x-on:member-joined.window="justJoined = true"
			class="bg-white rounded-lg px-4 py-6 relative text-rwp-widget-text shadow-card"
			x-bind:class="navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-stagger-fade-up rwp-stagger-1' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-stagger-slide-left rwp-stagger-1' : '')"
			data-become-member-section
		>
								<div x-show="showSuccess" style="display: none;">
					<div class="flex justify-center mb-4">
						<div class="size-16 bg-green-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
							<svg class="size-8 text-green-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
								<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
							</svg>
						</div>
					</div>
					<h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-2">
						Welcome to Vector Amino Labs Rewards!					</h3>
					<p class="text-sm mb-6 px-2">You&#039;re now part of our rewards program! Start earning points and rewards with every purchase.</p>

					<a
						x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : 'https://myaminolab.com/shop/'"
						x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
						x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
						x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
						class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline hover:bg-rwp-button-hover"
					>
						Start earning points					</a>
				</div>

								<div x-show="!showSuccess">
				<!-- Register Screen -->
				<div class="text-center" x-show="currentScreen === 'register'" x-transition:enter="transition-all duration-300 ease-out" x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 -translate-y-4 scale-95" x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100">
					<h2 class="text-base font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-2">Join our rewards program</h2>
					<p class="text-sm mb-4 px-2" x-show="!(isGuestMember && showGuestConfirmation)">Create a free account to start earning points and unlocking member-only perks.</p>
					<p class="text-sm mb-4 px-2" x-show="isGuestMember && showGuestConfirmation" x-cloak>Your order email will be used to create your account.</p>

				<!-- Form for non-logged-in users, guest members, and logged-in non-members - Initially hidden -->
				
						<!-- Guest confirmation: one-click account creation (no form fields needed, backend uses session) -->
						<div
							x-show="isGuestMember && showGuestConfirmation"
							x-transition:enter="transition-all duration-300 ease-out"
							x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 -translate-y-4 scale-95"
							x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100"
							class="space-y-3"
						>
							<!-- Email identity card -->
							<div class="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50/50 px-4 py-3 text-left">
								<svg class="size-5 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5"><desc>Envelope (email) icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M21.75 6.75v10.5a2.25 2.25 0 01-2.25 2.25h-15a2.25 2.25 0 01-2.25-2.25V6.75m19.5 0A2.25 2.25 0 0019.5 4.5h-15a2.25 2.25 0 00-2.25 2.25m19.5 0v.243a2.25 2.25 0 01-1.07 1.916l-7.5 4.615a2.25 2.25 0 01-2.36 0L3.32 8.91a2.25 2.25 0 01-1.07-1.916V6.75" /></svg>								<div class="min-w-0">
									<p class="text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-heading truncate" x-text="formData.email"></p>
									<p class="text-xs text-gray-500">From your recent order</p>
								</div>
							</div>

							<!-- Create account button -->
							<button
								type="button"
								tabindex="0"
								x-on:click="if (!isAdminPreview && !isDemo) { createMember() }"
								x-bind:disabled="isSubmitting"
								x-bind:class="isSubmitting ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : (isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? '!cursor-not-allowed' : 'hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover'"
								x-bind:data-preview-disabled="(isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? 'true' : null"
								x-bind:data-tippy-content="(isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? 'Disabled for preview' : null"
								class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center"
								data-highlight="success-state"
								data-become-member-btn
								x-bind:aria-label="isSubmitting ? 'Creating account...' : 'Create my account'"
							>
								<span
									class="[grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200"
									x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': isSubmitting, 'visible': !isSubmitting }"
								>Create my account</span>
								<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: isSubmitting !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: isSubmitting === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>							</button>

						</div>

						<!-- Full registration form (shown for non-guest visitors only) -->
						<form x-on:submit.prevent="if (!isAdminPreview && !isDemo) { createMember() }" x-show="showForm && !isGuestMember" x-transition:enter="transition-all duration-300 ease-out" x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 -translate-y-4 scale-95" x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100" x-transition:leave="transition-all duration-200 ease-in" x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100" x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 -translate-y-2 scale-98" style="display: none;">
								<div style="position:absolute;left:-9999px;top:-9999px;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;" aria-hidden="true">
			<label class="sr-only">
				Leave this field empty			</label>
			<input
				type="text"
				name="rwp_website"
				x-model="formData.rwp_website"
				value=""
				tabindex="-1"
				autocomplete="off"
			>
		</div>
								<div class="space-y-3">

							<div>
								<label for="member-name" class="sr-only block text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-text mb-1.5 text-left">Name</label>
								<input
									type="text"
									id="member-name"
									x-model="formData.name"
									class="bg-white text-base w-full px-3 py-3 rounded-lg outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200 placeholder:text-gray-400 focus:outline-2 focus:-outline-offset-2 focus:outline-blue-500"
									placeholder="Your name"
									required
								>
							</div>
							<div>
								<label for="member-email" class="sr-only block text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-text mb-1.5 text-left">Email</label>
								<input
									type="email"
									id="member-email"
									x-model="formData.email"
									class="bg-white text-base w-full px-3 py-3 rounded-lg outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200 placeholder:text-gray-400 focus:outline-2 focus:-outline-offset-2 focus:outline-blue-500"
									placeholder="Your email"
									required
								>
							</div>

							<button
								type="submit"
								tabindex="0"
								x-bind:disabled="isSubmitting"
								x-bind:class="isSubmitting ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : (isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? '!cursor-not-allowed' : 'hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover'"
								x-bind:data-preview-disabled="(isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? 'true' : null"
								x-bind:data-tippy-content="(isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? 'Disabled for preview' : null"
								class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center"
								data-highlight="success-state"
								data-become-member-btn
								x-bind:aria-label="isSubmitting ? 'Creating account...' : 'Create my account'"
							>
							<span
								class="[grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200"
								x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': isSubmitting, 'visible': !isSubmitting }"
							>Create my account</span>
							<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: isSubmitting !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: isSubmitting === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>						</button>
						</div>


					</form>
					
					<!-- Error State for Register Screen -->
					<div x-show="showError && currentScreen === 'register'" x-transition:enter="transition-opacity duration-300" x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0" x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100" style="display: none;" class="mt-4">
						<div class="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
							<div class="flex items-center">
								<svg class="size-5 text-red-400 mr-3 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
									<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path>
								</svg>
								<span class="text-sm text-red-700 text-left" x-html="errorMessage"></span>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>

			<button
				x-show="(!showForm || currentScreen !== 'register') && !(isGuestMember && showGuestConfirmation)"
				x-on:click="if (!isAdminPreview && !isDemo) { isGuestMember && userData.email && window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('rewardswp-join-with-email', { detail: { email: userData.email, name: userData.name } })); } currentScreen = 'register'; showForm = true; showError = false;"
				class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center"
				data-highlight="success-state"
				data-become-member-btn
				x-bind:aria-label="'Join now to become a member'"
				tabindex="0"
			>
				Join now			</button>

										<p class="text-sm mt-4">
						Already have an account?						<button
							x-on:click="currentScreen = 'login'; showForm = true; showError = false;"
							class="font-medium underline text-rwp-accent cursor-pointer"
							tabindex="0"
						>Sign in</button>
					</p>
										</div>

					<!-- Login Screen -->
					<div class="text-center" x-show="currentScreen === 'login'" x-transition:enter="transition-all duration-300 ease-out" x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 -translate-y-4 scale-95" x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100">
						<h2 class="text-base font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-2">Sign in to your account</h2>
						<p class="text-sm mb-6">Welcome back! Sign in to access your rewards.</p>

						<!-- Login Form -->
												<form x-on:submit.prevent="if (!isAdminPreview && !isDemo) { handleLogin() }" class="mb-4">
							<div class="space-y-3">
								<div>
									<label for="login-email" class="sr-only block text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-text mb-2 text-left">Email or Username</label>
									<input
										type="text"
										id="login-email"
										x-model="formData.email"
										class="bg-white text-base w-full px-3 py-3 rounded-lg outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200 placeholder:text-gray-400 focus:outline-2 focus:-outline-offset-2 focus:outline-blue-500"
										placeholder="Your email or username"
										required
									>
								</div>
								<div>
									<label for="login-password" class="sr-only block text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-text mb-2 text-left">Password</label>
									<input
										type="password"
										id="login-password"
										x-model="formData.password"
										class="bg-white text-base w-full px-3 py-3 rounded-lg outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200 placeholder:text-gray-400 focus:outline-2 focus:-outline-offset-2 focus:outline-blue-500"
										placeholder="Your password"
										required
									>
								</div>

								<button
									type="submit"
									tabindex="0"
									x-bind:disabled="isSubmitting"
									x-bind:class="isSubmitting ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : (isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? '!cursor-not-allowed' : 'hover:bg-rwp-button-hover'"
									x-bind:data-preview-disabled="(isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? 'true' : null"
									x-bind:data-tippy-content="(isAdminPreview || isDemo) ? 'Disabled for preview' : null"
									class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center"
								>
								<span
									class="[grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200"
									x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': isSubmitting, 'visible': !isSubmitting }"
								>Sign in</span>
							<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: isSubmitting !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: isSubmitting === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>							</button>

							</div>


						</form>
						
						<!-- Error State for Login Screen -->
						<div x-show="showError && currentScreen === 'login'" x-transition:enter="transition-opacity duration-300" x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0" x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100" style="display: none;" class="mb-4">
							<div class="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">

									<div class="relative size-6">

				<!-- Apply Coupon Button - Only show when NOT applied and NOT failed due to minimum spend -->

						}" class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center">
						<span class="[grid-area:stack] flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity duration-200" x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': applyingCoupon, 'visible': !applyingCoupon }">

							<template x-if="rewardType === 'free_product'">

						<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: applyingCoupon !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: applyingCoupon === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>					</button>

				</div>

								<div x-show="productAddedButCouponFailed" x-cloak class="mt-3 mb-3 text-sm w-full space-y-2">

'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl" x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }" class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block">

'javascript:void(0)' : 'https://myaminolab.com/checkout/'" class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block" x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }">
							Go to checkout						</a>

'javascript:void(0)' : 'https://myaminolab.com/cart/'" x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }" class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block">
								View cart							</a>

'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl" x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }" class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block">

			<!-- Form State -->

				<!-- Email Form -->
				<form x-on:submit.prevent="if (!isAdminPreview &amp;&amp; !isDemo) { claimReward() }" class="mb-3">
							<div style="position:absolute;left:-9999px;top:-9999px;opacity:0">
			<label class="sr-only">

			<input type="text" name="rwp_website" x-model="formData.rwp_website" value="">
		</div>

						<input type="email" id="claim-email" x-model="formData.email" class="bg-white text-base w-full px-3 py-3 rounded-lg outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200 placeholder:text-gray-400 focus:outline-2 focus:-outline-offset-2 focus:outline-blue-500" placeholder="you@example.com" required>

					</div>

					<!-- Error State -->
					<div x-show="showError" x-transition:enter="transition-opacity duration-300" x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0" x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100" class="mb-4">
						<div class="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
							<div class="flex items-center">
								<svg class="size-5 text-red-400 mr-3 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
									<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path>
