			style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>
		<!-- Bottom scroll fade — hidden when guest join section is visible -->
		<div x-show="!panelAtBottom && memberId !== 0"
			class="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-12 z-10"
			style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>

				<div x-show="memberId === 0">
		<!-- Fade Overlay -->
		<div
			class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-24 pointer-events-none"
			style="background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), var(--color-rwp-widget-bg) 20%, transparent);"
			x-data="{ hideJoinSection: false }"
			x-on:member-joined.window="hideJoinSection = true"
			x-show="!hideJoinSection"
		></div>

		<!-- Sticky Join Section -->
		<div
			class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-white px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-100"
			x-data="{ hideJoinSection: false, isLoggedIn: false }"
			x-on:member-joined.window="hideJoinSection = true"
			x-show="!hideJoinSection"
		>
	<div>
	<button
		type="button"
		x-on:click="window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-register-form'))"
		class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center"
	>
			Join now		</button>
		</div>
				<div x-show="!isLoggedIn" x-cloak class="mt-3 text-center text-rwp-widget-text">
					<p class="text-sm text-gray-600">
						Already have an account?						<button
							x-on:click="navigateToPanel('main', { resetScroll: true }); $dispatch('show-login-form');"
							class="font-medium underline text-rwp-accent cursor-pointer"
						>Sign in</button>
					</p>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
					</div>



				<div
			x-show="currentPanel === 'activity'"
			x-init="$watch('currentPanel', value => {
				if (value === 'activity') {
					if (activityItems.length === 0 || needsActivityRefresh) {
						if (needsActivityRefresh && activityTab === 'points') {
							activityItems = [];
							activityPage = 1;
							activityHasMore = false;
							activityCache = {};
						}
						loadActivity();
						needsActivityRefresh = false;
					}
				}
			})"
			x-bind:class="'relative h-full flex flex-col ' + (currentPanel === 'activity' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
		>
			<div class="shrink-0 px-6 pt-6 text-rwp-widget-text">
				<h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-3">Activity</h2>
				<!-- Segmented Control (hidden when only one tab is available) -->
				<div class="relative w-full mb-3" x-show="(pointsEnabled || (!!memberId && userData.points > 0)) && referralsEnabled">
					<div class="relative inline-grid items-center justify-center w-full h-10 grid-cols-2 p-1 text-gray-500 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl select-none" role="tablist">
						<button
							x-ref="activityPointsBtn"
							x-show="pointsEnabled || (!!memberId && userData.points > 0)"
							x-on:click="switchActivityTab('points')"
							type="button"
							class="relative z-20 inline-flex items-center justify-center w-full h-8 px-3 text-sm font-medium transition-all rounded-md cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
							x-bind:class="activityTab === 'points' ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-500'"
							role="tab"
							x-bind:aria-selected="activityTab === 'points'"
						>
							Points						</button>
						<button
							x-ref="activityReferralsBtn"
							x-show="referralsEnabled"
							x-on:click="switchActivityTab('referrals')"
							type="button"
							class="relative z-20 inline-flex items-center justify-center w-full h-8 px-3 text-sm font-medium transition-all rounded-md cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
							x-bind:class="activityTab === 'referrals' ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-500'"
							role="tab"
							x-bind:aria-selected="activityTab === 'referrals'"
						>
							Referrals						</button>
						<div class="absolute z-10 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm left-1 top-1 transition-transform duration-350 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.22,1.3,0.36,1)]" x-bind:style="'width: calc(50% - 4px); height: 32px; transform: translateX(' + ({'points': 0, 'referrals': 100}[activityTab] ?? 0) + '%)'"></div>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
			<div class="relative flex-1 min-h-0">
			<div x-ref="activityScroll" x-on:scroll.passive="syncActivityFade()" class="h-full overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 pt-1 pb-6 text-rwp-widget-text">

				<!-- Loading State -->
				<div x-show="activityLoading" class="flex items-center justify-center py-8">
					<div class="animate-spin size-6 border-2 border-gray-200 border-t-gray-600 rounded-full"></div>
				</div>

				<!-- Activity List -->
				<div x-show="!activityLoading">
					<!-- Empty State - Points Tab -->
					<template x-if="activityItems.length === 0 && !activityLoading && activityTab === 'points'">
						<div class="text-center py-8">
							<div class="flex justify-center mb-3">
								<svg class="size-10 text-gray-400" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
			<desc>
				Points activity icon
			</desc>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M12 2V1" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m12 23 0 -2.7661" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M1 12h2.71875" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M20.2812 12H23" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m6.0625 19.2258 -2.01636 2.0164" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m17.9375 5.03979 2.0164 -2.01635" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M6.0625 5.03979 4.04614 3.02344" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m17.9375 18.9602 2.0164 2.0164" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m12.4169 4.69626 1.9151 3.82921 3.6838 0.36877c0.0879 0.00861 0.1715 0.04201 0.2412 0.09631 0.0696 0.0543 0.1224 0.12727 0.1522 0.21041 0.0297 0.08314 0.0352 0.17302 0.0159 0.25918 -0.0193 0.08616 -0.0628 0.16505 -0.1252 0.22748L15.264 12.7225l1.1256 4.1238c0.0235 0.0869 0.0214 0.1787 -0.006 0.2644 -0.0275 0.0857 -0.0791 0.1616 -0.1486 0.2187 -0.0696 0.057 -0.1542 0.0928 -0.2436 0.1029 -0.0894 0.0101 -0.1798 -0.0058 -0.2604 -0.0458L12 15.5205l-3.73099 1.8679c-0.08052 0.0402 -0.17096 0.0562 -0.2604 0.0462 -0.08943 -0.0101 -0.17403 -0.0459 -0.24358 -0.103 -0.06955 -0.0571 -0.12107 -0.1332 -0.14833 -0.2189 -0.02727 -0.0858 -0.02911 -0.1776 -0.00531 -0.2644l1.1246 -4.1258 -3.03584 -3.03681c-0.06245 -0.06243 -0.10588 -0.14132 -0.12523 -0.22748 -0.01935 -0.08615 -0.01382 -0.17604 0.01595 -0.25918 0.02976 -0.08313 0.08254 -0.1561 0.15217 -0.21041 0.06964 -0.0543 0.15327 -0.0877 0.24115 -0.09631l3.68382 -0.36876 1.91509 -3.82922c0.0389 -0.07736 0.0985 -0.14238 0.1723 -0.18774 0.0737 -0.04536 0.1586 -0.06929 0.2452 -0.06909 0.0866 0.0002 0.1714 0.02452 0.2449 0.07022 0.0736 0.04571 0.1329 0.11099 0.1714 0.18854Z" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			</svg>							</div>
							<h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 mb-1" x-text="'No ' + currencyPlural + ' yet'"></h3>
							<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4" x-text="'See all the ways you can earn ' + currencyPlural"></p>
							<button
								x-on:click="navigateToPanel('ways-to-earn')"
								class="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 border border-transparent text-sm font-medium rounded-md text-white bg-rwp-brand hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover cursor-pointer transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-rwp-brand focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
							>
								<span x-text="'Earn ' + currencyPlural"></span>
							</button>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Empty State - Referrals Tab -->
					<template x-if="activityItems.length === 0 && !activityLoading && activityTab === 'referrals'">
						<div class="text-center py-8">
							<div class="flex justify-center mb-3">
								<svg class="size-10 text-gray-400" viewBox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
			<desc>
				Refer a friend icon
			</desc>
			<path d="m3.132 12.8 0.448 -1.175 -1.046 -2.744a0.929 0.929 0 0 1 0.36 -1.107l2.458 -1.605 0.767 -2.834a0.928 0.928 0 0 1 0.942 -0.684l2.932 0.149L12.279 0.955a0.928 0.928 0 0 1 1.165 0L15.73 2.8l2.932 -0.146a0.928 0.928 0 0 1 0.942 0.684l0.767 2.834 2.458 1.605a0.928 0.928 0 0 1 0.36 1.107l-1.045 2.744 1.045 2.743a0.929 0.929 0 0 1 -0.36 1.108l-2.458 1.6 -0.771 2.835a0.927 0.927 0 0 1 -0.942 0.685l-2.932 -0.146 -2.286 1.841" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m0.75 19.769 1.656 2.609" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m9.908 8.147 6.294 9.916" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="M15.871 17.541 2.075 21.857l-0.994 -1.566 9.158 -11.622 5.632 8.872z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			<path d="m5.056 20.924 0.876 1.346a2.041 2.041 0 0 0 2.906 0.614 2.256 2.256 0 0 0 0.587 -3.04l-0.163 -0.236" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path>
			</svg>							</div>
							<h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 mb-1">No referrals yet</h3>
							<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">When friends shop using your referral link, you&#039;ll see their activity here</p>
							<button
								x-on:click="navigateToPanel('main')"
								class="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 border border-transparent text-sm font-medium rounded-md text-white bg-rwp-brand hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover cursor-pointer transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-rwp-brand focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
							>
								Invite friends							</button>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Referral Summary Row (shown above activity items when referrals tab has data) -->
					<div x-show="activityTab === 'referrals' && activityItems.length > 0 && activityStatusCounts" class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-500 pb-2 mb-1">
						<span x-text="(activityStatusCounts ? activityStatusCounts.completed : 0) + ' completed'"></span>
						<span x-show="activityStatusCounts && !!activityStatusCounts.pending">&middot;</span>
						<span x-show="activityStatusCounts && !!activityStatusCounts.pending" x-text="(activityStatusCounts ? activityStatusCounts.pending : 0) + ' pending'"></span>
					</div>

					<!-- Activity Items -->
					<div class="divide-y divide-gray-100">
						<template x-for="item in activityItems" x-bind:key="item.id">
							<div class="py-3">
								<div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
									<!-- Left column: Description + Date -->
									<div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
										<!-- Referrals tab: Separate friend_name (semi-bold) and action_text (normal) -->
										<template x-if="activityTab === 'referrals' && item.friend_name">
											<p class="text-sm text-gray-900">
												<span class="font-semibold" x-text="item.friend_name"></span>
												<template x-if="item.action_text">
													<span class="font-normal" x-text="' ' + item.action_text"></span>
												</template>
											</p>
										</template>
										<!-- Points/other tabs: Primary line: Reward/action name -->
										<template x-if="activityTab !== 'referrals' || !item.friend_name">
											<p class="text-sm text-gray-900" x-text="item.target || item.action"></p>
										</template>
										<!-- Small text for order_id (refunds) -->
										<template x-if="item.order_id">
											<p class="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5" x-text="'#' + item.order_id"></p>
										</template>
										<!-- Small text for product_name (product reviews) -->
										<template x-if="item.product_name">
											<p class="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5" x-text="item.product_name"></p>
										</template>
										<!-- Secondary line: Date (all tabs) -->
										<p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5" x-text="item.date"></p>
									</div>
									<!-- Right column: Points value + currency OR Status pill -->
									<div class="flex-shrink-0 text-right">
										<!-- Points tab: Point value (with inline pending badge) + currency name below -->
										<template x-if="activityTab === 'points' && item.points !== undefined">
											<div>
												<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
													<p x-bind:class="item.source_type === 'points_expiration' ? 'text-gray-500' : (item.is_deduction ? 'text-gray-700' : (item.status === 'pending' ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-emerald-600'))"
														class="text-sm font-semibold"
														x-text="(item.is_deduction ? '-' : '+') + Math.abs(item.points).toLocaleString()">
													</p>
													<span x-show="item.status === 'pending'"
														class="text-[11px] text-gray-500">
														Pending													</span>
												</div>
												<p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5 text-right"
													x-text="getCurrencyName(Math.abs(item.points))">
												</p>
											</div>
										</template>
										<!-- Referrals/rewards tab: Status badge -->
										<template x-if="activityTab !== 'points' && item.status">
											<span
												x-bind:class="{
													'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-100': item.status === 'completed' || item.status === 'redeemed',
													'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-100': item.status === 'pending',
													'bg-gray-50 text-gray-700 border border-gray-200': item.status === 'issued',
													'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-100': item.status === 'blocked' || item.status === 'rejected',
													'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border border-gray-200': item.status === 'canceled'
												}"
												class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium mt-0.5"
												x-text="item.status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + item.status.slice(1)"
											></span>
										</template>
									</div>
								</div>
							</div>
						</template>
					</div>

					<!-- Load More -->
					<div x-show="activityHasMore" class="mt-4 text-center">
						<button
							x-on:click="loadMoreActivity()"
							x-bind:disabled="activityLoadingMore"
							class="text-sm text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 font-medium cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500"
						>
							<span x-show="!activityLoadingMore">Load more</span>
							<span x-show="activityLoadingMore" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
								<span class="animate-spin size-3.5 border-2 border-gray-200 border-t-gray-600 rounded-full"></span>
								Loading...							</span>
						</button>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
			<!-- Top scroll fade -->
			<div
				x-show="activityShowTopFade"
				class="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 h-8 z-10"
				style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"
			></div>
			<!-- Bottom scroll fade -->
			<div
				x-show="activityShowFade"
				class="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-12 rounded-b-2xl"
				style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"
			></div>
			</div>
		</div>

				<div
			x-show="currentPanel === 'create-account-prompt'"
			x-bind:class="'create-account-prompt-panel relative h-full flex flex-col ' + (currentPanel === 'create-account-prompt' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
		>
			<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 py-6 text-rwp-widget-text">
				<!-- Icon and Header -->
				<div class="text-center mb-6">

					<div class="relative inline-block mb-2">
						<!-- Main reward icon -->
						<div class="size-10 flex items-center justify-center text-rwp-widget-text/45" x-show="lockedReward" x-html="lockedReward?.iconHtml">
							<!-- Icon will be populated by Alpine -->
						</div>
					</div>
					<h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-3">
						<span x-show="lockedReward" x-text="lockedReward?.title"></span>
					</h2>
					<p class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text">
						Requires an account to redeem					</p>

				</div>

				<!-- Benefits List -->
				<div class="space-y-3 mb-8">
					<div class="flex items-start gap-3">
						<svg class="size-5 text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>						<div class="flex-1">
							<p class="text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-heading">
								Redeem rewards now							</p>
							<p class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text">
								Turn your <span x-text="currencyPlural.toLowerCase()"></span> into discounts and perks							</p>
						</div>
					</div>
					<div class="flex items-start gap-3">
						<svg class="size-5 text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>						<div class="flex-1">
							<p class="text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-heading">
								Instant setup							</p>
							<p class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text">
								One click and you&#039;re ready to go							</p>
						</div>
					</div>
											<div class="flex items-start gap-3">
							<svg class="size-5 text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>							<div class="flex-1">
								<p class="text-sm font-medium text-rwp-widget-heading">
									Earn even more								</p>
								<p class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text">
									Get +50 bonus <span x-text="currencyPlural.toLowerCase()"></span> for signing up								</p>
							</div>
						</div>
									</div>

		<!-- Create Account Button -->
		<button
			type="button"
			x-on:click="window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('show-register-form'))"
			class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center w-full"
		>
			Create account to use points		</button>

				<!-- Back Link -->
				<div class="text-center mt-4">
					<button
						type="button"
						x-on:click="navigateToPanel('ways-to-redeem')"
						class="text-sm text-rwp-widget-text hover:text-rwp-widget-text underline cursor-pointer"
					>
						Back to rewards					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- Redeem Success Panel -->
		<div
			x-show="currentPanel === 'redeem-success'"
			x-bind:class="'redeem-success-panel relative h-full flex flex-col ' + (currentPanel === 'redeem-success' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
		>
			<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 py-6 pb-4 text-rwp-widget-text">
				<!-- Success Header -->
				<div
					class="text-center mb-6"
					x-data="{
						cartSubtotal: 0,
						currencySymbol: '$'
					}"
				>
					<div class="flex justify-center mb-2">
						<svg class="size-10 text-green-600" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><desc>Check circle alternate</desc><path d="M6 13.223 8.45 16.7a1.049 1.049 0 0 0 1.707 0.051L18 6.828" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M0.75 11.999a11.25 11.25 0 1 0 22.5 0 11.25 11.25 0 1 0 -22.5 0Z" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path></svg>					</div>
					<p class="text-base text-gray-600 mt-2">
						Redeemed 						<span x-text="redeemSuccessData.points_cost"></span>
						<span x-text="' ' + redeemSuccessData.points_label"></span>
					</p>
					<h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 text-pretty" x-text="redeemSuccessData.title">
						<!-- Title populated from Alpine data -->
					</h2>
					</div>

						<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
				<div class="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center mb-2 px-2">
					<p>Use this coupon on your next order</p>
				</div>
			</template>

						<div>
								<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
					<div class="mb-4" x-data="copyHandler()">
						<div x-data="{ isCheckmark: false }" class="flex rounded-lg bg-white outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200">
							<input
								type="text"
								readonly
								x-ref="couponCode"
								x-bind:value="redeemSuccessData.couponCode"
								x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, null, false)"
								class="block min-w-0 grow px-5 py-3 text-sm font-mono text-gray-900 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none sm:text-sm/6 text-ellipsis"
							>
							<div class="flex">
								<button
									x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data)"
									class="py-3 px-3 relative transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
									x-bind:class="{ 'hover:text-gray-900 text-gray-500': !isCheckmark}"
									x-ref="copyButton">
									<div class="relative size-6">
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
											x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-0 scale-95' : 'opacity-100 scale-100'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="-1 -1 24 24"><desc>Copy to clipboard icon</desc><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M17.875 6.645833333333333h2.520833333333333c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666V17.875" stroke-width="2"></path><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M0.6875 1.6041666666666665c0 -0.5062658333333333 0.41041 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666h12.833333333333332c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666v-12.833333333333332Z" stroke-width="2"></path></svg>										</div>
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
											x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-100 scale-100' : 'opacity-0 scale-95'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>										</div>
									</div>
								</button>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</template>

				<!-- Button and messages section -->
				<div>

				<!-- Cart-aware Button Section -->
				<div
					x-data="couponUtils( {
						nonce: '480d6bf443',
						buttonText: 'Apply to cart',
						rewardId: redeemSuccessData.reward_id || 0,
						couponCode: redeemSuccessData.couponCode || '',
						isApplied: redeemSuccessData.is_applied || false,
						cartItemCount: 0,
						cartProductIds: [],
						cartSubtotal: 0,
						freeProductId: redeemSuccessData.free_product_id || null,
						minimumOrderAmount: redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount || 0,
						integrationSource: 'woocommerce',
						checkoutUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/checkout/',
						cartUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/cart/',
						storeUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/shop/',
						isAdminPreview: false,
						isDemo: false,
						addToCartText: 'Add to cart',
						claimRewardText: 'Claim reward',
						applyToCartText: 'Claim free product',
						currencySymbol: '$',
						pendingRewardId: 0,
						dismissedRewardId: 0					} )"
					x-on:reset-buttons.window="if ( $event.detail.except !== $el ) isApplied = false;"
				>
									<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending && (redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount)">
						<p class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 text-center mb-2"></p>
					</template>

									<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending && redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount > 0 && cartSubtotal < redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount">
						<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 text-center text-pretty mb-2" x-text="'Claim now — we\'ll add it to your cart when you spend ' + currencySymbol + redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount + ' or more'"></p>
					</template>

									<button
						x-show="!isApplied && !isPending && !productAddedButCouponFailed"
						class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center"
						x-on:click="applyCoupon( redeemSuccessData.couponCode, redeemSuccessData.reward_id )"
						x-bind:disabled="isApplying"
						x-bind:class="{
							'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed': isApplying,
							'hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover': !isApplying
						}"
					>
						<span
							class="[grid-area:stack] flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity duration-200"
							x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': isApplying, 'visible': !isApplying }"
						>
							<!-- Dynamic text based on reward type, cart state, minimum spend, and integration -->
							<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product'">
								<span x-text="getFreeProductButtonText(redeemSuccessData.free_product_id, redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount)"></span>
							</template>
							<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
								<span>
							<span x-show="isWooCommerce">Apply to cart</span>
							<span x-show="!isWooCommerce">Apply coupon</span>
								</span>
							</template>
						</span>
							<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: isApplying !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: isApplying === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>						</button>

					<div x-show="hasErrors" class="text-sm mt-2 text-red-500">An error occurred. Please reload the page and try again.</div>

					<!-- Success Message - Show when coupon applied -->
					<div x-show="isApplied" x-cloak class="mb-1 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-emerald-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
						<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product'">
							<span>Free product added to cart</span>
						</template>
						<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
							<span x-text="redeemSuccessData.title + ' applied'"></span>
						</template>
					</div>

										<template x-if="isPending && !productAddedButCouponFailed">
						<div class="text-sm w-full">
							<!-- Confirmation badge -->
							<div class="font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
								<svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
									<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
								</svg>
								<span>Reward claimed</span>
							</div>

							<!-- Explanation text -->
							<div class="text-center text-gray-500 px-2 mb-3" x-text="'We\'ll add it to your cart when you spend ' + currencySymbol + redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount">
							</div>

							<!-- Action buttons -->
							<div class="space-y-3">
								<!-- Primary CTA - Start/Continue Shopping -->
								<a
									x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
									x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
									x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
									x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
									x-text="isCartEmpty ? 'Start shopping' : 'Continue shopping'"
								></a>

								<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only, when cart has items) -->
								<template x-if="isWooCommerce && !isCartEmpty">
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
										class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
									>
										View cart									</a>
								</template>
							</div>
						</div>
					</template>

										<div x-show="productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending" x-cloak class="mt-3 mb-3 text-sm w-full space-y-2">
						<div x-show="productWasNewlyAdded" class="text-center text-emerald-600 font-medium">
							Product added to cart						</div>
												<template x-if="remainingToUnlock > 0">
							<div class="text-center text-gray-600 px-2" x-text="'Almost there — add $' + remainingToUnlock + ' more to unlock'"></div>
						</template>
					</div>

					<!-- Buttons when coupon failed but product added (fallback) -->
					<template x-if="productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending">
						<div>
							<!-- Primary CTA - Continue Shopping -->
							<a
								x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
								x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
								x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
								x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
								class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
							>
								Continue shopping							</a>

							<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
							<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
								<a
									x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
									x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
									x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
									x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
								>
									View cart								</a>
							</template>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Error Message - Show when coupon failed to apply (no product involved) -->
					<div x-show="hasErrors && !productAddedButCouponFailed" x-cloak class="mb-1 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-red-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
						<span x-text="errorMessage"></span>
					</div>

					<!-- Buttons when coupon is applied successfully -->
					<template x-if="isApplied && !isCartEmpty">
						<div>
							<!-- Primary CTA - Go to Checkout -->
							<a
								x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : checkoutUrl"
								x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
								x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
								class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
								x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
							>
								Go to checkout							</a>

							<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
							<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
								<a
									x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
									x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
									x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
									x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
								>
									View cart								</a>
							</template>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Button when coupon is applied but cart is empty -->
					<template x-if="isApplied && isCartEmpty">
						<div>
							<!-- Primary CTA - Shop Now -->
							<a
								x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
								x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
								x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
								x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
								class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
							>
								Shop now							</a>
							<!-- No secondary button when cart is empty -->
						</div>
					</template>

				<!-- Coupon Terms (below buttons) - show only expiry when applied or ready (minimum met). -->
				<template x-if="redeemSuccessData.coupon_terms">
					<div class="mt-2 text-center">
						<span class="text-sm text-gray-500" x-text="(isApplied || (redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isPending && (redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount))) && redeemSuccessData.expiry_text ? redeemSuccessData.expiry_text : redeemSuccessData.coupon_terms"></span>
					</div>
				</template>
				</div>
				</div>

			</div>

			</div>

						<div
				x-data="{
					couponFailed: false,
					showCode: false,
					isCheckmark: false,
					isPending: false,
					isApplied: false,
					cartSubtotal: 0,
					minimumOrderAmount: 0,
					pendingRewardId: 0,
					...copyHandler()
				}"
				x-init="
					minimumOrderAmount = redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount || 0;
					isPending = pendingRewardId > 0 && pendingRewardId === parseInt(redeemSuccessData.reward_id || 0, 10);
					window.addEventListener('free-product-coupon-failed', () => { couponFailed = true; });
					window.addEventListener('show-redeem-success', () => { couponFailed = false; isPending = false; isApplied = false; });
					window.addEventListener('reward-set-pending', () => { isPending = true; });
					window.addEventListener('reward-auto-applied', () => { isPending = false; isApplied = true; });
					window.addEventListener('coupon-applied', () => { isApplied = true; });
				"
				x-show="redeemSuccessData.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && (isPending || (redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= redeemSuccessData.minimum_order_amount))"
				x-cloak
				class="px-6 text-center bg-gray-50 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-col-reverse items-center"
			>
					<button
						x-on:click="if (!showCode) { showCode = true; $nextTick(() => { handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data); }); } else { showCode = false; }"
						class="text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-600 underline underline-offset-2 cursor-pointer bg-transparent border-0 my-4"
						x-text="showCode ? 'Hide code' : 'Use a code at checkout'"
					></button>

					<!-- Collapsible code section -->
					<div x-show="showCode" x-collapse x-cloak class="w-full">
						<div class="pt-4">
						<div class="flex rounded-lg bg-white outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200">
							<input
								type="text"
								readonly
								x-ref="couponCode"
								x-bind:value="redeemSuccessData.couponCode"
								x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, null, false)"
								class="block min-w-0 grow px-5 py-3 text-sm font-mono text-gray-900 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none sm:text-sm/6 text-ellipsis"
							>
							<div class="flex">
								<button
									x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data)"
									class="py-3 px-3 relative transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
									x-bind:class="{ 'hover:text-gray-900 text-gray-500': !isCheckmark}"
									x-ref="copyButton">
									<div class="relative size-6">
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
											x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-0 scale-95' : 'opacity-100 scale-100'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="-1 -1 24 24"><desc>Copy to clipboard icon</desc><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M17.875 6.645833333333333h2.520833333333333c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666V17.875" stroke-width="2"></path><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M0.6875 1.6041666666666665c0 -0.5062658333333333 0.41041 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666h12.833333333333332c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666v-12.833333333333332Z" stroke-width="2"></path></svg>										</div>
										<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
											x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-100 scale-100' : 'opacity-0 scale-95'">
											<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>										</div>
									</div>
								</button>
							</div>
						</div>
						</div>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>


				<div
			x-show="currentPanel === 'your-rewards'"
			x-bind:class="'relative h-full flex flex-col overflow-hidden ' + (currentPanel === 'your-rewards' ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
			x-data="{ panelAtTop: true, panelAtBottom: false }"
						x-effect="if (currentPanel === 'your-rewards') { void availableRewards?.length; const el = $el.querySelector('.overflow-y-auto'); if (el && el.scrollHeight > 0) { panelAtBottom = el.scrollTop + el.clientHeight >= el.scrollHeight - 5; } }"
		>
			<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-0 py-6"
				@scroll="panelAtTop = $el.scrollTop < 5; panelAtBottom = $el.scrollTop + $el.clientHeight >= $el.scrollHeight - 5"
			>

			<h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-rwp-widget-heading mb-3 px-6">Your rewards</h2>

			<div class="px-3">
				<template x-if="availableRewards && availableRewards.length > 0">
					<template x-for="reward in availableRewards" x-bind:key="reward.id">
						<div>
							<button
								x-on:click="navigateToPanel('reward-detail-' + reward.id)"
								class="w-full hover:bg-gray-50 text-left px-4 py-3 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 !px-3"
									>
							<div class="flex items-center gap-4">
								<div class="size-8 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-rwp-widget-icon-text" x-html="reward.icon_html">
									<!-- Icon will be populated from server -->
								</div>
								<div class="flex-1">
									<div class="font-medium text-rwp-widget-heading text-sm" x-text="reward.title"></div>
									<div class="text-xs text-gray-500">
										<template x-if="reward.is_out_of_stock">
											<span class="text-amber-600">Out of stock</span>
										</template>
										<template x-if="!reward.is_out_of_stock && reward.points_cost > 0">
											<span x-text="'Redeemed ' + reward.points_cost + ' ' + reward.points_label"></span>
										</template>
										<template x-if="!reward.is_out_of_stock && reward.points_cost === 0">
											<span>From referral</span>
										</template>
									</div>
								</div>
							</div>
										<svg class="size-5 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5">
											<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M8.25 4.5l7.5 7.5-7.5 7.5" />
								</svg>
								</button>
							</div>
						</template>
				</template>

				<!-- Empty state when no rewards -->
					<template x-if="!availableRewards || availableRewards.length === 0">
						<div class="text-center py-8">
							<div class="flex justify-center mb-3">
								<svg class="size-10 text-gray-400" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><desc>Free product icon</desc><g><g><path d="M15.75 23.24a3 3 0 0 0 -3 -3H9a3 3 0 0 0 -3 -3H0.75v6Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="m6 20.24 3 0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path></g><path d="M16.5 17.74H21a0.76 0.76 0 0 0 0.75 -0.75v-6.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="m8.25 10.24 0 4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M7.5 5.99h15s0.75 0 0.75 0.75v2.75s0 0.75 -0.75 0.75h-15s-0.75 0 -0.75 -0.75V6.74s0 -0.75 0.75 -0.75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="m15 15.24 0 -9.25" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M19.27 3.87A11.84 11.84 0 0 1 15 6a11.93 11.93 0 0 1 2.12 -4.24C18.46 0.43 19.21 0.63 19.8 1.22s0.78 1.33 -0.53 2.65Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path><path d="M10.73 3.87A11.84 11.84 0 0 0 15 6a11.93 11.93 0 0 0 -2.12 -4.24C11.54 0.43 10.79 0.63 10.2 1.22s-0.78 1.33 0.53 2.65Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5"></path></g></svg>							</div>
							<h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 mb-1">No rewards yet</h3>
							<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-5">Start earning to unlock your first reward</p>
							<div class="flex justify-center">
								<button
									x-on:click="navigateToPanel('ways-to-earn')"
									class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 py-2 px-4 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-rwp-button-hover"
								>
									Earn Points								</button>
							</div>
						</div>
					</template>
				</div>
			</div>

			<!-- Top scroll fade -->
			<div x-show="!panelAtTop"
				class="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 h-8 z-10"
				style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>
			<!-- Bottom scroll fade -->
			<div x-show="!panelAtBottom"
				class="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-12 z-10"
				style="right: var(--rwp-scrollbar-width, 8px); background: linear-gradient(to top, var(--color-rwp-widget-bg), transparent);"></div>
		</div>

				<template x-for="reward in availableRewards" x-bind:key="'detail-' + reward.id">
			<div
				x-show="currentPanel === 'reward-detail-' + reward.id"
				x-bind:class="'reward-detail-panel relative h-full flex flex-col ' + (currentPanel === 'reward-detail-' + reward.id ? (navigationDirection === 'initial' ? 'rwp-panel-fade-up' : (navigationDirection === 'forward' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-forward' : (navigationDirection === 'back' ? 'rwp-panel-slide-back' : ''))) : '')"
			>




				<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar px-6 py-6 pb-6 text-rwp-widget-text">
					<!-- Icon and Title (Centered like friend claim) -->
					<div
						class="text-center mb-6"
						x-data="{
							cartSubtotal: 0,
							currencySymbol: '$'
						}"
					>
						<div class="flex justify-center mx-auto mb-2 [&>svg]:size-10" x-html="reward.icon_html">
							<!-- Icon will be populated from server -->
						</div>
						<h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 text-pretty" x-text="reward.title"></h2>
					</div>

										<template x-if="reward.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
						<p class="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center mb-2" x-text="reward.description"></p>
					</template>

			<!-- Coupon Code Display with Copy Functionality -->
			<template x-if="reward.coupon_code && reward.coupon_code !== ''">
					<div>
												<template x-if="reward.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
							<div class="mb-4" x-data="copyHandler()">
								<div x-data="{ isCheckmark: false }" class="flex rounded-lg bg-white outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200">
									<input
										type="text"
										readonly
										x-ref="couponCode"
										x-bind:value="reward.coupon_code"
										x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, null, false)"
										class="block min-w-0 grow px-5 py-3 text-sm font-mono text-gray-900 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none sm:text-sm/6 text-ellipsis"
									>
									<div class="flex">
										<button
											x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data)"
											class="py-3 px-3 relative transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
											x-bind:class="{ 'hover:text-gray-900 text-gray-500': !isCheckmark}"
											x-ref="copyButton">
											<div class="relative size-6">
												<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
													x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-0 scale-95' : 'opacity-100 scale-100'">
													<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="-1 -1 24 24"><desc>Copy to clipboard icon</desc><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M17.875 6.645833333333333h2.520833333333333c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666V17.875" stroke-width="2"></path><path stroke="currentColor" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M0.6875 1.6041666666666665c0 -0.5062658333333333 0.41041 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666h12.833333333333332c0.506275 0 0.9166666666666666 0.41041 0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666v12.833333333333332c0 0.506275 -0.41039166666666665 0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666 0.9166666666666666h-12.833333333333332c-0.5062658333333333 0 -0.9166666666666666 -0.41039166666666665 -0.9166666666666666 -0.9166666666666666v-12.833333333333332Z" stroke-width="2"></path></svg>												</div>
												<div class="absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out"
													x-bind:class="isCheckmark ? 'opacity-100 scale-100' : 'opacity-0 scale-95'">
													<svg class="size-6" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><desc>Checkmark icon</desc><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>												</div>
											</div>
										</button>
									</div>
								</div>
							</div>
						</template>

						<!-- Button and messages section -->
						<div>

					<!-- Out of Stock Notice -->
					<template x-if="reward.is_out_of_stock">
						<div class="text-center mb-4 p-4 bg-amber-50 rounded-lg">
							<p class="text-sm text-amber-700 font-medium">
								This product is currently out of stock							</p>
							<p class="text-xs text-amber-600 mt-1">
								Your reward will remain available when the product is back in stock.							</p>
						</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Cart-aware Button Section -->
					<div
						x-data="couponUtils( {
							nonce: '480d6bf443',
							buttonText: 'Apply to cart',
							rewardId: reward.id,
							couponCode: reward.coupon_code || '',
							appliedButtonText: 'Coupon applied',
							isApplied: reward.is_applied || false,
							isOutOfStock: reward.is_out_of_stock || false,
							cartItemCount: 0,
							cartProductIds: [],
							cartSubtotal: 0,
							freeProductId: reward.free_product_id || null,
							minimumOrderAmount: reward.minimum_order_amount || 0,
							integrationSource: 'woocommerce',
							checkoutUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/checkout/',
							cartUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/cart/',
							storeUrl: 'https://myaminolab.com/shop/',
							isAdminPreview: false,
							isDemo: false,
							addToCartText: 'Add to cart',
							claimRewardText: 'Claim reward',
							applyToCartText: 'Claim free product',
							currencySymbol: '$',
							pendingRewardId: 0,
							dismissedRewardId: 0						} )"
						x-on:reset-buttons.window="if ( $event.detail.except !== $el ) isApplied = false;"
					>
												<template x-if="reward.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending && (reward.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= reward.minimum_order_amount)">
							<p class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 text-center mb-2"></p>
						</template>

											<template x-if="reward.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending && reward.minimum_order_amount > 0 && cartSubtotal < reward.minimum_order_amount">
							<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 text-center text-pretty mb-2" x-text="'Claim now — we\'ll add it to your cart when you spend ' + currencySymbol + reward.minimum_order_amount + ' or more'"></p>
						</template>

											<button
							x-show="!isApplied && !isPending && !isOutOfStock && !productAddedButCouponFailed"
							class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 grid-rows-1 [grid-template-areas:&#039;stack&#039;] place-items-center"
							x-on:click="applyCoupon( reward.coupon_code )"
							x-bind:disabled="isApplying"
							x-bind:class="{
								'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed': isApplying,
								'hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover': !isApplying
							}"
						>
							<span
								class="[grid-area:stack] flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity duration-200"
								x-bind:class="{ 'invisible': isApplying, 'visible': !isApplying }"
							>
								<!-- Dynamic text based on reward type, cart state, minimum spend, and integration -->
								<template x-if="reward.reward_type === 'free_product'">
									<span x-text="getFreeProductButtonText(reward.free_product_id, reward.minimum_order_amount)"></span>
								</template>
								<template x-if="reward.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
									<span>
								<span x-show="isWooCommerce">Apply to cart</span>
								<span x-show="!isWooCommerce">Apply coupon</span>
									</span>
								</template>
							</span>
							<svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin [grid-area:stack] transition-opacity duration-200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x-bind:class="{ &#039;invisible&#039;: isApplying !== true, &#039;visible&#039;: isApplying === true }"><desc>Loading spinner icon</desc><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>						</button>

						<div x-show="hasErrors" class="text-sm mt-2 text-red-500">An error occurred. Please reload the page and try again.</div>

						<!-- Success Message - Show when coupon applied -->
						<div x-show="isApplied" x-cloak class="mb-1 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-emerald-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
							<template x-if="reward.reward_type === 'free_product'">
								<span>Free product added to cart</span>
							</template>
							<template x-if="reward.reward_type !== 'free_product'">
								<span x-text="reward.title + ' applied'"></span>
							</template>
						</div>

												<template x-if="isPending && !productAddedButCouponFailed">
							<div class="text-sm w-full">
								<!-- Confirmation badge -->
								<div class="font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
									<svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
										<path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
									</svg>
									<span>Reward claimed</span>
								</div>

								<!-- Explanation text -->
								<div class="text-center text-gray-500 px-2 mb-3" x-text="'We\'ll add it to your cart when you spend ' + currencySymbol + reward.minimum_order_amount">
								</div>

								<!-- Action buttons -->
								<div class="space-y-3">
									<!-- Primary CTA - Start/Continue Shopping -->
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
										class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
										x-text="isCartEmpty ? 'Start shopping' : 'Continue shopping'"
									></a>

									<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only, when cart has items) -->
									<template x-if="isWooCommerce && !isCartEmpty">
										<a
											x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
											x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
											x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
											x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
											class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
										>
											View cart										</a>
									</template>
								</div>
							</div>
						</template>

						<!-- Partial Success Message - Product added but coupon failed (fallback for edge cases) -->
						<div x-show="productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending" x-cloak class="mt-3 mb-3 text-sm w-full space-y-2">
							<div x-show="productWasNewlyAdded" class="text-center text-emerald-600 font-medium">
								Product added to cart							</div>
														<template x-if="remainingToUnlock > 0">
								<div class="text-center text-gray-600 px-2" x-text="'Almost there — add $' + remainingToUnlock + ' more to unlock'"></div>
							</template>
						</div>

						<!-- Buttons when coupon failed but product added (fallback) -->
						<template x-if="productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending">
							<div>
								<!-- Primary CTA - Continue Shopping -->
								<a
									x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
									x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
									x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
									x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
								>
									Continue shopping								</a>

								<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
								<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
										class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
									>
										View cart									</a>
								</template>
							</div>
						</template>

						<!-- Error Message - Show when coupon failed to apply (no product involved) -->
						<div x-show="hasErrors && !productAddedButCouponFailed && !isPending" x-cloak class="mb-1 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-red-600 text-sm w-full justify-center">
							<span x-text="errorMessage"></span>
						</div>

							<!-- Buttons when coupon is applied successfully -->
							<template x-if="isApplied && !isCartEmpty">
								<div>
									<!-- Primary CTA - Go to Checkout -->
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : checkoutUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
										x-bind:class="{ 'mb-3': isWooCommerce, '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
									>
										Go to checkout									</a>

									<!-- Secondary CTA - View Cart (WooCommerce only) -->
									<template x-if="isWooCommerce">
										<a
											x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : cartUrl"
											x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
											x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
											x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
											class="text-sm inline-block w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 px-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 no-underline block"
										>
											View cart										</a>
									</template>
								</div>
							</template>

							<!-- Button when coupon is applied but cart is empty -->
							<template x-if="isApplied && isCartEmpty">
								<div>
									<!-- Primary CTA - Shop Now -->
									<a
										x-bind:href="isAdminPreview ? 'javascript:void(0)' : storeUrl"
										x-bind:data-preview-disabled="isAdminPreview ? 'true' : null"
										x-bind:data-tippy-content="isAdminPreview ? 'Disabled for admin preview' : null"
										x-bind:class="{ '!cursor-not-allowed': isAdminPreview }"
										class="bg-rwp-brand text-rwp-widget-button-text text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-rwp-brand-hover disabled:hover:bg-rwp-brand disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-500 w-full py-3 px-8 min-h-[48px] mx-auto flex items-center justify-center no-underline block"
									>
										Shop now									</a>
									<!-- No secondary button when cart is empty -->
								</div>
							</template>

						<!-- Coupon Terms (below buttons) - show only expiry when applied or ready (minimum met). -->
						<template x-if="reward.coupon_terms">
							<div class="mt-2 text-center">
								<span class="text-sm text-gray-500" x-text="(isApplied || (reward.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isPending && (reward.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= reward.minimum_order_amount))) && reward.expiry_text ? reward.expiry_text : reward.coupon_terms"></span>
							</div>
						</template>
						</div>
						</div>

					</div>
					</template>

					<!-- Back to Your Rewards Button -->



				</div>

								<div
					x-data="{
						couponFailed: false,
						showCode: false,
						isCheckmark: false,
						isPending: false,
						isApplied: false,
						cartSubtotal: 0,
						minimumOrderAmount: 0,
						pendingRewardId: 0,
						...copyHandler()
					}"
					x-init="
						minimumOrderAmount = reward.minimum_order_amount || 0;
						isApplied = reward.is_applied || false;
						isPending = pendingRewardId > 0 && pendingRewardId === parseInt(reward.id || 0, 10);
						window.addEventListener('free-product-coupon-failed', () => { couponFailed = true; });
						window.addEventListener('show-redeem-success', () => { couponFailed = false; isPending = false; });
						window.addEventListener('reward-set-pending', () => { isPending = true; });
						window.addEventListener('reward-auto-applied', () => { isPending = false; isApplied = true; });
						window.addEventListener('coupon-applied', () => { isApplied = true; });
					"
					x-show="reward.reward_type === 'free_product' && !isApplied && (isPending || (reward.minimum_order_amount <= 0 || cartSubtotal >= reward.minimum_order_amount))"
					x-cloak
					class="px-6 text-center bg-gray-50 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-col-reverse items-center"
				>
					<button
						x-on:click="if (!showCode) { showCode = true; $nextTick(() => { handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data); }); } else { showCode = false; }"
							class="text-sm text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 underline underline-offset-2 cursor-pointer bg-transparent border-0 my-4"
							x-text="showCode ? 'Hide code' : 'Use a code at checkout'"
						></button>

						<!-- Collapsible code section -->
						<div x-show="showCode" x-collapse x-cloak class="w-full">
							<div class="pt-4">
							<div class="flex rounded-lg bg-white outline-1 -outline-offset-1 outline-gray-200">
								<input
									type="text"
									readonly
									x-ref="couponCode"
									x-bind:value="reward.coupon_code"
									x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, null, false)"
									class="block min-w-0 grow px-5 py-3 text-sm font-mono text-gray-900 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none sm:text-sm/6 text-ellipsis"
								>
								<div class="flex">
									<button
										x-on:click="handleCopyClick($refs.couponCode, $data)"
										class="py-3 px-3 relative transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
										x-bind:class="{ 'hover:text-gray-900 text-gray-500': !isCheckmark}"
										x-ref="copyButton">
										<div class="relative size-6">
